### 21.1 拉普兰德's first refresh; 突变细胞 is not consumed and its new operator joins the bench (#1, #4) — `sim/content/garrisons/meta.js SERVER_GAIN_BOND_LAYER_BY_REFRESH_CNT`, `match/PlayerState.js pieceRoundCount / bumpPieceRoundCount / _mergeChess / transformChess / acquireChess`, `match/effectsMeta.js ctx.pieceCounter / incPieceCounter / transform`, `match/builtinMeta.js char_chess_transformation_equip`, `match/audit.js`, `match/bot.js cellTarget / makeHandRoom`, `tools/build-data.mjs ITEM_RULES`, `ui/detailPanel.js ItemDetail`

- **#1 seen**: with 普罗旺斯 + 德克萨斯 deployed — refresh, buy 拉普兰德, deploy her (3 叙拉古), refresh again: no 叙拉古 layers. Players: officially "获得该干员后该回合的首次刷新" also stacks. **Cause**: the handler compared the PLAYER's round refresh count with `refresh_cnt` 1.
- **#1 official**: garrison_123_a/_b = SERVER_REFRESH_SHOP / SERVER_GAIN_BOND_LAYER_BY_REFRESH_CNT, bb `refresh_cnt` 1, layer 4 / 8, "若为本回合首次主动刷新…此干员在整备区时也有效" (activity_table act2autochess). Prep effects run on the scene server (research 11 §1.2); PRTS has no timing note. Text and report agree when the count belongs to the trait instance; the "本回合每刷新过1次" multipliers (阿罗玛 122, 安洁莉娜 147) fire on other events and keep the player's count.
- **#1 now**: each copy counts the manual refreshes it sees this round, on the board or in the hand (`piece.meta.round`, module-prefixed key `garrison:SERVER_GAIN_BOND_LAYER_BY_REFRESH_CNT:refreshes`), and fires on the `refresh_cnt`-th. A new piece starts at 0; a move or an in-place promotion keeps the count; a re-triggered trait neither fires nor counts; temp pieces do not count.
- **#4 seen**: "昆图斯策略给的突变细胞用完就没了". **Cause**: research 04's [ASSUMED] "item consumed"; the built-in and a content override both destroyed the cell.
- **#4 official**: the text "战斗结束后，装备者替换为高一阶的随机干员" never says 销毁 (every consumable's text does; data EQUIP, upgradeNum 100); PRTS 下半/PRTS盟约记录 备注 "生效时，原干员销毁，获得一名高一阶的随机初始干员（最高六阶）"; PRTS 卫戍协议/帮助 "佩戴的装备无法手动卸除，在失去该干员（干员出售、销毁、合并等）或装备合并为进阶品质时自动卸除"; community guides re-inject it every round (bilibili cv47000418, cv48003106; cg.163.com "这个道具可以无限使用").
- **#4 where the new operator goes** (corrected after the WA merge; pointed out in the closed PR #2, whose playtest claim the footage confirms): official gameplay footage — bilibili BV1vzyVBuEN9 (上半, ≈ 8:24) and BV1Qkw1zMEoR (下半, ≈ 7:25) — shows the next prep with the carrier's tile **empty**, 剩余可放置角色 one higher and the 整备区 holding the returned equipment and the new tier+1 operator, which the player then deploys by hand. It reads the PRTS 备注 as written — a destroy followed by a gain ("原干员销毁，获得一名…") — and PRTS 帮助 sends whatever a player recruits or gains to the 手牌区 (= 整备区; "被发送至手牌区的物资优先从右到左填充空位"). WA had kept the new operator on the carrier's tile when legal [ASSUMED] and counted that tile for a merge the transformation completed [ASSUMED]; both are withdrawn (PR #2 itself still let the merge use the carrier's tile — not adopted).
- **#4 now**: after every battle the carrier is destroyed wherever it stands (board, hand or temp — a board tile is freed and the deploy count drops; its summons are removed, its copies return to the pool). Its equipment, the cell included, returns to the hand **first** (overflow temp; auto-merging like any gain). Then a random NORMAL operator one tier higher (max 6; an elite carrier too) is gained like any gained operator (`PlayerState.transformChess` → `acquireChess`, onGain source `'transform'`): the hand (right → left), overflow temp, and with both full the no-room rule of every gain (back to the pool, toast 整备区已满，获得的干员已返还; an item that found no slot takes one the gain freed, else stays on the gained operator up to 2 slots, else it is destroyed with a log warning, as in a merge). Like any operator on the bench it brings no summon card until it is deployed. A gain that completes a merge merges like any gained copy (§20.11): the elite on a consumed **deployed copy's** tile, else the hand — the carrier's freed tile is no copy's (`_mergeChess` lost its `fromKey` / `fromDir` options, `audit.js` its exception for them); the returned cell is stowed before the elite's summon card. Per-piece state stays with the returned items (商业包装方案's sell count, keyed by the item). One implementation (the built-in); the item card states the rule (items.json `note`). Bots inject their least valuable single normal operator below 6阶 (`cellTarget`: never an elite, a merge pair or a carrier of a cell), make a hand slot for a cell left in temp (`makeHandRoom`), and — AI 托管 too — deploy the gained operator in the next prep's lineup step like any owned unit (a real co-op round in the tests: SETTLE → the next prep). The buy loop's pre-emptive bench shed, which sells the weakest bench single by piece value while the bench is crowded, no longer sells a piece gained since the bot's last prep ended (`bot.js rememberOwned`): on the new rule it had sold 44 of 222 gained operators in 20 solo 绝境 matches on 昆图斯 before any placement step saw them (now none; a gained operator stays off the board only when the board is full). Measured (`tools/botbench.mjs`, decisions only): strategy 昆图斯 (`--band band_quintus`, seeds 201–220) 绝境 8 / 20 wins on the WA build → 7 with the bench rule alone → 11 with the shed fix (LP left 16.8 → 13.3 → 17.1), 险境 15 → 16 → 17 (16.3 → 16.2 → 17.6); the bots' own strategies, bench rule alone → with the fix: solo 绝境 (seeds 1–12) 8 → 8 / 12, solo 险境 (seeds 13–36) 19 → 19 / 24, co-op 绝境 4 AI (seeds 1–36) 9 → 8 / 36 (LP left within 0.6) — noise; the shed's cost is a set of the owned uids per prep. The client needs no special case: `render/app.js setPrep` drops the carrier's view and draws the new piece on its bench slot (checked in headless Chrome: the tile empty, the operator and the cell on the bench, no promotion cue, no console error).
- [ASSUMED]: an elite merged this round keeps its copies' highest count (no second firing that round); a copy re-bought after a sale fires on its own first refresh ("获得该干员后"; each extra +4 costs 3 + 1 − 1 = 3 funds); a bench carrier (hand or temp) transforms too (the footage shows deployed carriers); a 6阶 carrier becomes another 6阶; with the hand and temp both full the gained operator and an item without a slot follow the rules of every gain (no source shows that case).
- Tests: `test/match/feedback1-meta.test.js` (a deployed and a bench carrier, the hand full → temp, the hand and temp full, a merge with and without a deployed copy, 商业包装方案's count, bots and AI 托管 redeploying, a real solo 绝境 昆图斯 bot match: no gained operator shed in its first prep, none waiting on the bench beside a free deployment), `test/ui/feedback1-cell.test.js`; `items.test.js`, `merge.test.js`, `facing.test.js`, `playtest6-elite-to-board.test.js` updated.

### 21.2 Bounty drafts by the official round structures; special offers shown as what they hold (#2, #6) — `tools/build-data.mjs BOUNTY_INITIAL_SETS / BOUNTY_BOSS_GROUPS / BOUNTY_HUNTER_GROUPS / SHOP_DRAFT / TACTIC_DRAFT`, `match/choices.js bountyDraftCards / shopDraftCards / tacticDraftCards`, choices.json `bountyDrafts` / `shopDraft` / `tacticDraft` / `draftPool` / schedule `bountyDraft`, `test/fixtures/official-bounty-drafts.json`; `match/PlayerState.js pushRewardOffer / pushItemOffer`, `match/effectsMeta.js offerLabel`, `ui/gameLogic.js offerHeader`, `ui/shopBar.js RewardCards`, `ui/rewardOverlay.js`

- **#2 seen**: "本来应该后期出的悬赏的怪物在前期的悬赏就出现了，导致选了打不过". **Cause**: every draft drew uniformly from 105 cards (§20.6), so R3 could offer the R9 bosses and the R11 giants.
- **#2 official** (66 screenshots of 22 co-op 绝境 / 终极 matches of this season at R3 / R9 / R11, collected by the user 2026-10-03; readings in the fixture, no player names): every card is an act2autochess (下半) effect whose enemy is the effect's `enemy_id` (the title only names category and tier: 悬赏·损伤I = 底海滑动者 e12_4 / 临时收音师 e18_1 / 心虚设计师 e16_11); each 悬赏决策 event is a fixed card list and a draft shows 6 different cards of it in varying positions.
  - **R3 (22 / 22)**: six 接下来两场作战 cards, I I I II II III (10 coins), one of 10 fixed sets (enemy_initial_1..10), all six shown; 9 sets seen — series 17 / 18 / 19 whole, and hand-made mixed sets (one holds two series-20 cards, one holds 自制投石机 e17_6), so no simple rule describes them.
  - **R9 (22 / 22)**: boss bounties X·悬赏 + 源石虫·特训, 下场作战 — one of 6 groups (one per bossInitial event) of 9, 9, 9, 9, 8 and 6 cards; 庞贝 and 鼠王 never share a draft; named bosses do not always come together (match 19 = 自在 + 杰斯顿 + 陷落雪祀, match 12 = 杰斯顿 alone). The 鼠王 group came in 14 of 22 matches, every dark-grey-board match among them.
  - **R11**: 悬赏决策 14, 机密商店 4, 战术决策 4, 道具补给 0 of 22. A bounty draft is a list of 7 下场战斗 cards — one 特异III giant, one card per faction series — of which the draft leaves out one (match 15 leaves out the giant; matches 1 / 6 / 21 / 22 leave out four different cards of one list); 7 lists seen.
  - **机密商店** (matches 2, 4, 5, 8): six free items with repeats (盟约之币 ×2, 变形同构体 ×2); each shop exactly two tier VI, at least one V and one 盟约之币, no tier I / II. **战术决策** (matches 7, 9, 18, 20): six ally cards each (no 排斥 / 责罚 / 裁决, no 模拟战场演变), 补给 twice in match 7.
  - Never in a draft: the 7 multi-round cards (the 假想敌 question is answered), enemyeffect_3_*, 战术特训, the 鸭爵 set.
- **#2 now**: cards.bounty `draftPool` initial 42, boss 20, hunter 24 (86 drafted); `draftExcluded` 'unseen' (19: multi-round, e3_*, 凋零骑士 / 遗弃者 / 锏 / 扎罗 / 迷路的巨像). A draft = one group, then 6 different cards of it, each drawn with weight 1 + the official drafts of its group it showed in. R3 = one of 10 slots: a seen set, or for the unseen 10th a rule set (tiers I I I II II III, ≤ 2 per series, the III 法术大师A1 e10_6 or 鼎沸 e20_6 — the two-battle cards no draft showed); R9 = a seen group, picked by the matches it came in; R11 = one of the 7 seen lists, uniform (the 4 lists seen with 6 cards completed by the list shape, so 12_8 / 16_2 / 16_6 come in about 1 R11 bounty draft in 15); 绝境 / 终极 R11 families: bounty 14 / shop 4 / tactic 4 (the official counts; was supply 50 / shop 50). The R11 机密商店 (`shopDraft`): six slots drawn with replacement — VI, VI, V, 盟约之币 and twice V / IV / III / 盟约之币 — items weighted by the official cards they showed on; the earlier 标准 / 险境 shops keep any tier I–VI per card, also with replacement. The 战术决策 (`tacticDraft`): every card drawn on its own with replacement; at R11 only the ally cards, weighted 1 + the official cards each showed on; other rounds every card, uniform. Identical cards are separate cards (picks go by index; two picks of a team card stack). `MULTI_ROUND_BOUNTY_BATTLES = 2` stays (it now only affects 教鞭's 法术大师A2·多轮战术特训). The mode's inactive enemy list no longer removes bounty cards (PRTS 11/18 note "不影响悬赏决策出场"). Flip: add a newly seen set, group or list to the build-data constants and rebuild. On the 22 official bounty drafts the generator reaches every one (60 000 seeds per round, `mode_multi_hard` and `mode_multi_abyss`).
- **#6 seen**: 凯瑟琳's three items appeared as blank operator cards with raw ids under 晋升奖励. **Now**: a special-refresh offer is drawn as what it holds — free item cards (icon, name, tier, description, FREE) or chess cards under its source's label (凯瑟琳 定向投放, 娜仁图亚 见者有份, 寻呼模块, 信标, 松果) + SPECIAL + 免费选择 1 件 / 名; m.private `shop.rewardOffer = { tier, source: 'merge'|'special', label, queued, slots }`; offers waiting behind the shown one show as "之后还有 N 项" and a +N on the reminder pill; a special offer plays the refresh sound.
- [ASSUMED]: the card weights inside a group; the unseen 10th R3 set (rule) and 险境 R6 drafting like R3; R9 groups picked by the matches they came in (what of the match decides — map, leader or difficulty — is open) and the three single-draft groups completed only with the base cards they lack; R11 = one of the 7 seen lists (which of the 15 events R11 fires is open: by the blocks of effectChoiceInfoDict it would be bounty_hunter_8..15, and with 8 events an unseen list would come in about 1 R11 bounty draft in 8); the R11 family weights = the official counts; the 机密商店 slot split and item weights, and the earlier 标准 / 险境 shops; the 战术决策 weights, R11 without terrain cards, independent draws (open: all 4 official drafts hold a 驰援, a 盟誓 and two of 列装 / 财富 / 补给 / 整备 / 升华), twin battle passives stacking; solo 绝境 / 终极 R11 = the co-op weights (no solo screenshot; the data has 15 solo `bounty_hunter_*_s` events); solo shows 3 of 6.
- Tests: `test/match/feedback1-bounty.test.js`, `test/match/feedback1-secret-shop.test.js`, `test/match/feedback1-tactic.test.js`, `test/ui/feedback1-offers.test.js`, `test/ui/feedback1-secret-shop.test.js`, `test/ui/feedback1-tactic.test.js`, `test/match/playtest6-matchflow.test.js`, `test/ui/playtest6-bounty.test.js`, `test/content/choices.test.js`; opt-in `test/ui/feedback1-offers.e2e.test.js`, `feedback1-secret-shop.e2e.test.js`, `feedback1-tactic.e2e.test.js` (`SP_E2E=1`).

### 21.3 No deployment in the water; tactical points inside the tactician's range (#3, #9) — `sim/grid.js DEPLOY_REFUSED_TILES / canStand`, `tools/build-data.mjs effectiveBuildable / ownerRange`, `match/board.js ownerRangeKeys`, `match/PlayerState.js _legal / summonRange / _liftOutOfRange / _reorient`, `ui/gameLogic.js summonRange`, `match/bot.js`, `match/invariants.js`, `content/kits/tier3.js tacticalPoint`

- **#3 seen**: "有水池的那张图干员可以错误的被部署到水里，突袭分队干员也会自动落到水里" — the 深水区 (`tile_deepsea`) of 战场#08(下半) 涨潮控制 (act2 m04): board (10–12, 6), partner (10–12, 14), boss (3–5, 6) / (3–5, 14). **Cause**: the level gives it buildableType ALL and the legend copied it; the deploy maps (server, client, bots) and `grid.canStand` (the 突袭 landing tile, tactical points, summon tiles) treated it as road, and a tactical point even preferred it (it lies on the enemy path).
- **#3 official**: PRTS 深水区, 地形信息 tile_deepsea: 部署类型 全部位, 地形机制 "拒绝部署（待补充）"; PRTS 作战机制: the 地形标记 AdvancedBuildableMask (values include 深水) "限制玩家仅能部署匹配的单位于其上"; `trap_040_canoe` 特制水上平台 "在水上建立可以部署任意单位的平台" (season 1's 战场#05 puts one on each water tile). There is no PRTS page "地形".
- **#3 now**: `DEPLOY_REFUSED_TILES` makes the effective buildable NONE: stages.json `tiles.d.buildable` NONE (`buildableType` ALL kept), `deployTiles` without the water, `canStand` refuses it (and melee units on a hard-blocked 射击台 / mound); every automatic placement and the bots follow. An active 特制水上平台 makes its tile deployable in the prep deploy map only (act1 m05, weight 0; the sim models no canoe, its Grid keeps those tiles NONE [ASSUMED]). Gate routes are unchanged; the water counts as non-blockable in the path tie-break, so a few off-route smoothed steps of the m04 boss field (rows 4–5) go around it. 维娜·维多利亚's lions skip it too (§21.8).
- **#9 seen**: "干员伺夜这样的战术家的战术点能被错误的被放到攻击范围之外". **Official**: 狼群 / 流形 token text "只能部署在召唤者攻击范围内" (character_table; PRTS 狼群 特性); the tactician trait "可以在攻击范围内选择一次战术点来召唤援军"; PRTS 卫戍协议/帮助 "移动干员时，其所属召唤物全部退场并重置至手牌区".
- **#9 now**: tokens.json `ownerRange`; such a piece stands only on `ownerRangeKeys(loadout attack grid, owner tile, owner dir)` — enforced by the server (`_legal` / `summonRange`), the client (drag highlights; toast "只能部署在召唤者攻击范围内"), the bot and the invariants. Moving the owner sends its summons back (unchanged); in swaps each piece is checked from the tile it ends on; an in-place re-orientation or promotion that leaves the summon outside sends it back to its stack with a toast — one still inside stays (user-confirmed, §21.20); with nowhere to go (hand and temp full) the re-orientation is refused with `HAND_FULL`, on the other paths the summon leaves the board and comes back at the next round start (`grantTokensFor`). No out-of-range placement reaches the battle.
- [ASSUMED]: the range is the loadout's grid (`attackRangeGrid`, §21.16; items and bonds do not widen it); automatic placements never put a melee unit on a 射击台; the canoe in the deploy map only.
- Tests: `test/match/feedback1-placement.test.js`, `test/sim/feedback1-water.test.js`; board, playtest5-deploy, playtest6_summons, ui/gameLogic, pathing-blockable / -crosscheck updated; opt-in `test/ui/feedback1-placement.e2e.test.js` (`SP_E2E=1`; fastServer `SP_STAGE`).

### 21.4 Enemies that change form when knocked out (#5 转译基底·α, #8 深池逐火) — `content/enemies.js` (husk, kitTranslator, reborn, statue, setForm, rebirthCleanse), `sim/ai.js enemyAttack`, `sim/snapshot.js`, `shared/protocol.js fxForm`, `battle/runner.js keepsState / hold / compactHeld`, `screens/game.js keepEarly`, `render/interp.js isCosmeticEvent`, `render/app.js renderInfo`, `render/units.js FORMS`, `render/spine.js`

- **#8 seen**: the 怨恨的余烬 could not be killed. **Cause**: the ember stood still, 隐匿 AND unblockable, so no operator could ever target it (a real R3 round on 战场#01: 0 of 7 killed, 7 leaked).
- **#8 official** (PRTS 深池逐火战士 / 护卫 天赋; `Revive[Trigger]` 5 / 10, `interval` 10; PRTS 特殊机制 §重生 / §特殊生命值机制; 作战机制 §隐匿): every knock-out → a 1 s 重生 (immobile, 无敌 + 无法阻挡 + 失衡免疫); then the ember — max HP 5 (护卫's 火灰 10), 1 per damage instance, no attack, 隐匿 + 缴械; not killed within 10 s → the warrior with full HP. A blocked enemy loses 隐匿.
- **#8 now**: the ember walks on; blocked, it is targetable and drawn solid (the stealth bit only while its 隐匿 is on). The same round: 7 / 7. An operator's radius area damage reaches it only while blocked or revealed (§21.19).
- **#5 seen**: 转译基底 played a "transform" clip and vanished. **Causes**: no clip sets for its forms (it stayed on A_* and died on B_Die, the 寻仇者's death clip); its first form took damage; and the client dropped the change fx in catch-up frames, hidden tabs, the pre-entry buffer and the render engine's stale-event drop (b.snap carries no form).
- **#5 official** (PRTS 转译基底·α 天赋; talents Passive / Mode_*_Passive / Mode_Fuchou_Anger): no attack; every damage instance cancelled; 失衡免疫 + control immunities; the 4th physical hit → 寻仇者, the 4th arts hit → 特战术师 (2 targets), a block → 幽灵 (unblockable, no attack — PRTS 幽灵 "不进行普通攻击，无法被阻挡"); once, 2 s.
- **#5 now**: A_Die_B / _C / _D, then B_* / D_* / C_*; no HP lost before the change (an HP loss stops at 1); the armed forms attack through `ai.js enemyAttack` (`e.profile`).
- **Kill accounting**: a survived knock-out gives no kill, credit, bounty, die event or death hook; wave completion and 联防 counters see the enemy standing; a bounty coin is paid once, at the final death.
- **重生** (PRTS §重生 "清空自身身上除白名单外所有Buff"): `rebirthCleanse` removes ally-sourced buffs and source-less statuses, keeping `persist` talents, enemy-given buffs (锏's 抵抗) and source-less field state; its skills restart from their initial cooldown when it ends (§21.19).
- **Forms on the client**: `setForm` keeps `e.form` (UnitInfo `form`) and puts `form` on its fx (`fxForm`, the 'phase' fx too, so barrier / charge phases are no forms). No client stage drops a form fx: runner catch-up frames (`keepsState`), the hidden-tab backlog of the battle on screen (`hold`; past `HELD_MAX` superseded status / skill toggles are compacted away, `compactHeld`, and only a backlog still past it re-enters the view from the field meta), a battle that ended while hidden (delivered when the tab is shown; every batch keeps the game time it was drained at, `EV_SLICE`), the game screen's pre-entry buffer (`keepEarly`, also while a re-sent field meta re-enters the field on screen) and the render engine's queue (`render/interp.js isCosmeticEvent`: a form fx is never dropped as stale nor shed; one handed out late switches the model without its telegraph or a change clip that would already have ended, its closing clip shortened — the last three since the integration, §21.19). Late views: `renderInfo` keeps `form`. Closing clips are timed from the fx `dur` and keep the current form's death clip until the next form's fx.
- **Audit**: 锏 / 扎罗 had #5's look (fixed: their 重生 clips); 杰斯顿 → C2_*; 守墓石像 per PRTS (statue, then flight, §21.9); 再生: 1 s 重生, then shields within 1.8. No unit stays untargetable. “余音” stays unmapped (no source says which form is A).
- [ASSUMED]: the translator is immobile during its change and stays damage-cancelled; the HP-loss floor 1; form letters B / C / D; the 特战术师 form also hits flyers (the standalone PRTS 特战术师 "不会攻击飞行单位" may carry over — one line, `canTarget: (u) => !u.isFlying`); the ember's 10 s counts from the end of the 重生; 再生's 傀儡 walks; the statue does not attack (clip Sleep); 隐匿 returns at once after a block (PRTS: 3 s); the 重生 whitelist.
- Tests: `test/sim/feedback1-transform.test.js`, `test/match/feedback1-runner-forms.test.js` (stalls, hidden tabs, a battle ending while hidden, compaction, the runner feeding the render buffer), `test/render/interp.test.js`, `test/render/forms.test.js`, `test/content/enemies_bosses.test.js`; opt-in `test/render/feedback1-forms.browser.test.js` (`RENDER_E2E=1`; blocking 1 s long tasks).

### 21.5 The 鸭爵 strategy's enemies (#7) — `content/enemies.js` (runWhenHit, 圆仔 kits), `content/bands/meta.js duckReplace / duckGlobal`, `Match.startFinalAssault` (event `routes` / `side`), `tools/build-data.mjs bandSwapEnemyKeys`

- **Seen**: "鸭爵策略带来的怪物圆仔错误的能被阻挡"; the user asked for an audit of the other three. **Cause**: 圆仔's kit had only the front guard (its unblockability is in PRTS's talent text, not in the handbook ability list). The audit found more: 鸭爵's run multiplied the speed by `run.attack@move_speed` instead of adding it; the swap ran only in normal battles and only on NORMAL-rank enemies; the four kept the database's roguelike `lifePointReduce` 0; on a pair boss field a player's swaps came from both halves.
- **Official**: PRTS 圆仔 天赋 "无法攻击/被阻挡；受到来源于正面的物理和法术伤害-80%；自身始终朝向我方干员数量最多的方向"; PRTS 鸭爵 天赋 "受到伤害时开始奔跑，移动速度+400%"; PRTS 卫戍协议：盟约 下半/PRTS盟约记录 §策略 鸭爵 备注: "每回合将有0~2名敌人被替换为上述敌人之一，且上述敌人为特殊版本，相较于原版更弱" (popup: 鸭爵 6000 HP, +300 % speed when hurt; 高普尼克 11000 / 1600 ATK; 流泪小子 4500, 3.5 s stun; 圆仔 13000), "但进入保护目标点将减少1点目标生命值，且在最终回合和隐秘核心回合中仍然生效". Data: the band's enemylist names the `_2` versions (blackboard min 0, max 2, round 5, minweight 0.6, maxweight 0.99). How the swap is drawn is in no source.
- **Now**: 圆仔 / 圆仔_2 unblockable + front guard; 鸭爵 / 鸭爵_2 run ×(1 + v) once (+400 % / +300 %). The swap runs in normal, Final Assault and Hidden Core battles (联防 adds none); candidates: any ground wave enemy, normal or elite, except the leader, its parts, bounties, earlier swaps and uncounted units — each swap takes the replaced unit's time and route; on a pair boss field each player's 0–2 come from the enemies whose route ends on that player's half (`ev.side` + `ev.routes`, the bounties' half rule), the 60–99 % share taken over that half. The four `_2` enemies have `lpr` 1 (builder): the Final Assault / Hidden Core LP and the card's 目标价值 read it.
- [ASSUMED]: the uniform 0–2 count and the uniform pick; minweight / maxweight = the 60–99 % part of the player's enemies in time order; ground only; elites eligible (about two thirds of the leader rounds' escorts in that share are elites; NORMAL-only would leave ≈ 80 % of R14 pair halves without a candidate vs ≈ 36 %); per-half swaps; decided at battle start (not in the prep preview); 圆仔's backward pose not rendered.
- Tests: `test/match/feedback1-ducklord.test.js` (a real R6 wave; solo Final Assault + LP; co-op Final Assault → Hidden Core pair fields on the real match path), `test/content/bands.test.js`, `test/content/enemies_bosses.test.js`, `test/data.test.js`.

### 21.6 AI players (#10 "人机有点太笨了") — `server/match/bot.js`, `tools/botbench.mjs`

- **Measured on the 0.1.0 bot** (`tools/botbench.mjs`, same seeds on two builds): 0–2 refreshes a prep and side-grade singles sold again for 1 (≈ 2 merges a match, ≈ 2 elites at R13); a level-up could spend the funds a third copy needed; the bounty score ignored the enemy (co-op 绝境: 155 of ≈ 310 bounty enemies leaked); 信标 (destroys its carrier) went on the best operator; DPS ignored DEF / RES; the focus bond flip-flopped.
- **Now** (decisions only, no rule or balance change): refresh vs buy by the shop odds of completing held pairs (a full board buys only merge progress or a lineup upgrade worth more than the refreshes its price would pay for; an elite's value includes the merge's free pick); ≤ 4 pairs on the bench, merges before level-ups, a freeze for an unaffordable third copy; one committed focus core bond + a second bond, a teammate's main core bond read from its public bond strip (humans too) counting against it; dealers valued by DPS against the round's DEF / RES; 坎诺特 keeps its interest capital (5) back; bounty picks by expected payout − expected leaks × (2 + 20 / LP) with the kill chance from the exposure model — an expected-value pick (co-op leaks are bound by the drafts, §21.2); items by effect (信标 on a bench single, 拟态物质 on a pair, both 博士投影 on the strongest normal operator, 突变细胞 per §21.1, bond items on a focus member, survival items on blockers); 教鞭 / “神秘顾客” used after a perfect battle and kept otherwise — a bot drops a kept one only for a hand slot, a human's seat under AI 托管 never; a tactician's 援军 inside `PlayerState.summonRange`.
- **Result** (seeds 1–40, rehearsal 3, on the WF branch): 独立 绝境 19 → 26 / 40 wins (LP left 14.3 → 16.6), 同盟 绝境 (4 AI) 5 → 12 / 40 (5.1 → 12.7), 标准 unchanged; merges per bot ≈ 2 → 3–4; forced strategies (solo 绝境, seeds 201–220): 坎诺特 9 → 14, 昆图斯 9 → 11, 杜宾 8 → 9. **Decision time unchanged**: heuristics CPU p50 / p95 31 / 74 → 28 / 70 ms solo and 17 / 39 → 17 / 38 ms co-op; the whole prep with its rehearsal 89 / 205 and 83 / 162 ms wall.
- **Reconciled with the merged rules** (§21.19): the server's legality (deploy map without the 深水区, `summonRange`), WA's cell rule (and its destroy-then-gain correction: the gained operator is deployed by the lineup step, the bench shed spares a piece gained since the last prep — §21.1), WB's draft structures (it scores whatever is offered), 本局禁用 bonds (never planned), the range a unit is deployed with, and the AoE of §21.18 in its DPS value. On the merged build, seeds 1–40: solo 绝境 27 → 27 wins, co-op 绝境 9 → 11 (within the noise); decision time stays (heuristics CPU 24 / 57 ms solo, 16 / 36 ms co-op; the whole prep 78 / 153 and 73 / 151 ms wall; BALANCE §5). After the QA: WB's 22-match bounty lists (a giant in every R11 bounty list, 14 of 22 R11s a 悬赏决策) take the same bot to 22 / 40 and 9 / 40 — a rule change; the bench-shed fix (never sell a piece of this prep, §21.19) then 24 / 40 and 10 / 40. **The 0.1.1 build against 0.1.0 (19 / 40, 5 / 40): 24 / 40 solo 绝境, 10 / 40 co-op 绝境 (4 AI)** — part of it is the rules: 0.1.0's bot on the 0.1.1 rules wins 23 / 40 and 7 / 40 (LP left 14.6 / 5.2 against 17.4 / 13.1, merges per bot 2.2 / 2.0 against 3.8 / 3.1); same-prep buy → sell in co-op 绝境 (2 AI 托管 + 2 bots, seeds 21–26) 29 → 3; decision time unchanged (one thread, seeds 1–3: the whole prep 81 / 187 → 81 / 184 ms wall solo, 71 / 153 → 71 / 129 ms co-op; heuristics CPU 36 / 88 → 35 / 86 and 16 / 40 → 16 / 40 ms).
- [ASSUMED] (tuned; the official AI is undocumented): MERGE_HIT 36, MAX_PAIRS 4, BENCH_BUDGET 6, FOCUS_MEMBER 4, ARMOR_WEIGHT 10, BOUNTY_KILL 2, the LP value 2 + 20 / LP, the teammate penalty 6, the 坎诺特 reserve = the band's interest capital, `CROWD` (×2 / ×1.3 / ×1.4).
- Tests: `test/match/bot-smarter.test.js` (bounty picks, item carriers, Arts kept / AI 托管 safe, tactician range, pair via buy or freeze, 坎诺特, the bond strip, the merged rules, a CPU cost guard < 300 ms), `test/match/feedback1-meta.test.js` (AI 托管 never destroys an item to clear temp).

### 21.7 奥术 works; 本局禁用 bonds; the 源石溶剂 merge (B1, B2) — `ui/gameLogic.js modeOffBonds`, `ui/detailPanel.js BondChips`, `ui/shopBar.js ChessCard`, `ui/bondStrip.js BondPopup`, `screens/game.js`, `match/PlayerState.js _mergeChess`, `match/effectsMeta.js destroyPiece`, `content/bonds/addon/battle.js`

- **B1 measured — the bond works** (a real 险境 battle through `Match._normalOpts`, and the same battle from its BattleSpec, the browser path — identical): each member's arts hit debuffs its target ×(1.2 + 0.01·L) arts damage taken for 3 s and later arts hits land multiplied (深靛 + 洛洛, R5, seed 7: 41 / 54 arts hits debuffed at L0, 0 without the bond); 3 different members: below 50 % HP ×1.4·(…) (seed 7: 9 hits at ×1.68 and, after 海霓's live +3 layers, 19 at ×1.722); all 10 members × S1–S3 × normal / elite apply it, summons do not (not 干员); leaders too (Final Assault 绝境 R14, 999 谢拉格: 329 819 → 426 911 leader damage in 700 s at L0).
- **What a player saw — fixed**: 标准模拟 switches 奥术 off (official modeDataDict inactiveBondIdList: 拉特兰 阿戈尔 卡西米尔 灵巧 奥术 奇迹 投资人 突袭 独行 绝技), while 深靛 洛洛 阿罗玛 夕 圣聆初雪 stay in the pool through their other bond; their cards showed 奥术 normally, the chip read 「0/2 未激活」 with three deployed and the popup 「在场 0/2 未激活」. **Now** such a bond is struck through on shop / reward cards, the chip reads 本局禁用, the popup reads 本局禁用 with a note and no 在场 count or 当前效果 block (its numbers would promise an effect) [ASSUMED look].
- **Official, unchanged**: 限伤 cancels a leader hit ≥ 300 000 in boss / hidden battles (§20.12), so very large multiplier stacks can make huge hits vanish (巴哈姆特 12316); our measured boards stay far below it (largest 24 813).
- **The stacking rule, reconciled — §20.10 stands**: PRTS 盟约记录's current note 「※同一单位仅可对同一目标同时施加1个该盟约法术伤害提升效果」, read with the engine default (PRTS 常见同名状态) and 巴哈姆特 12316, gives one effective instance per target (applyStrongest). Known deviation [ASSUMED]: an earlier revision (oldid 386936) said a unit cannot re-apply while its own instance lasts; an equal re-hit refreshes it here (slightly stronger). Strongest rather than earliest stays [ASSUMED].
- **B2 — not reproduced**: every PlayerState path ends with exactly one golden and no white (buy next to a white in the hand or equipped, two buys, equip on another or a full carrier, a reward pick, 道具补给, 列装, 整备 + buy, 画卷, the carrier's sale); random intents over all 50 mergeable items conserve W + 2·G and never leave a pair (upgradeNum 2 → _b; PRTS 帮助 「已拥有2件同一初始装备时…自动合并」). A latent gap closed: equipment returned by a promotion (`_mergeChess`) or by `ctx.destroyPiece` (突变细胞, the round-start promotion item) now runs `checkItemMerges` (unreachable by a player before).
- [ASSUMED]: 整备 bought next to an owned white merges the two first and keeps the effect for the next purchase (the official text may mean the bought item turns golden); the 标准 popup lists members the mode keeps out of the pool dimmed as not owned.
- Tests: `test/sim/feedback1b-arcane.test.js`, `test/ui/feedback1b-bonds.test.js`, `test/match/feedback1b-items.test.js`.

### 21.8 维娜·维多利亚's lions; 玛恩纳 S3 hits air units (B3, B4) — `kits/tier6.js siege2`, `tokens.js SKILL_SUMMON_UNCAPPED`, `kits/tier5.js` 玛恩纳

- **B3 seen**: her S3 (default, defaultSkillIndex 2) summoned one 黄金盟誓. **Real**: on the real match path (战场#05 下半, R8, (10,5)) every cast placed 1 lion beside 3 free melee tiles (the reviewer's sweep of m01–m04: 74 casts, all 1 lion). The kit picked one tile, and the per-owner deploy limit (the token's phase maxDeployCount 1, a hand count) would have withdrawn a second.
- **B3 official**: skill_table skchr_siege2_3 "立即在天赋一生效范围内可部署地面召唤“黄金盟誓”"; EN client "Summons Golden Vows on deployable tiles within Talent 1's range"; wiki.gg "only be spawned on open low ground tiles"; PRTS 备注 "于技能结束时/维娜退场时自动退场".
- **B3 now**: one lion on every free tile of her 3×3 talent area where a melee piece could deploy (LOW, ALL / MELEE, unoccupied, not a reserved home tile; `grid.canStand`, so never the 深水区), at most 8, for the skill's duration; `SKILL_SUMMON_UNCAPPED` keeps the deploy limit off them; each counts for 诸王的叹息 (546 × (1 + 1.45 + 0.30) with 6 lions). 1 → 3 lions at (10,5) on 战场#05, 6 at (11,4) on 战场#01.
- **B4 seen**: 玛恩纳 "开技能没伤害". **Real, on flying waves**: his default S3 fires (CUSTOM_RANGE 3-18) on any enemy, flyers included, but his attacks were ground-only melee — a flyer-only cast hit nothing for 26 s, then the trait ramp reset (4 of 19 real casts; seed 21 R2 dealt 0).
- **B4 official** (PRTS S3 备注): "※可对空"; "每击倒一名符合条件的敌人，特性提供的攻击力加成-10%，特性最低降低至+0%" — only "自身普通攻击（与该次攻击附带的伤害）" kills count, "于当次攻击后统一结算", not kills by 第二天赋 (reflection), by a mark another unit's attack set off, or sourceless ones; S2's "不重置" follows the same rule.
- **B4 now**: S3 `targeting.canHitFly` (flyer-only casts 0–10k → 14k–93k damage); bonus = max(0, ramp × trait_up − 0.1 × own kills), settled after the attack; an own kill = an enemy his current attack (or the mark it set off) damaged — the set is cleared after each attack, so carried damage counts and reflection / teammates' marks do not. His trait sets block 0 only while the skill is off: during S2 / S3 he blocks up to 3, and the 卡西米尔 bond's true damage rides his attacks only while he blocks nothing.
- [ASSUMED]: fence tiles (low, deployable, not walkable) count as 可部署地面; "−10 %" = absolute points of the bonus.
- Tests: `test/content/feedback1b_kits.test.js`, `kits_t6` (every lion off the reserved tile), `test/sim/feedback1-integrate.test.js` (no lion in 战场#08's 深水区).

### 21.9 Air units are 静态刚体 (B5) — `tools/build-data.mjs STATIC_BODIES`, enemies.json `staticBody`, `sim/Battle.js _displaceable`, `content/bosses.js` (胄 parts), `content/enemies.js` (statue, 喷气人)

- **Seen** (relayed: "飞机可以被薄绿的技能拉走（难道不能拉动吗？…你去复核下）"). **Real**: `_displaceable` checked only 失衡免疫 and leaders, so every flyer moved by the 力度 − 重量 tables — 薄绿 S2 dragged 妖怪 1.33 tiles per hit and an act2 m01 drone reached the goal 6.6 s late; with client combat drones were dragged 43–55 times per match; 崖心 S1 moved a 刺胄之弹 0.87 tiles; 守墓石像 moved 0.03–0.44 tiles (1.4–1.7 under 失重).
- **Official**: PRTS 特殊机制 静态刚体 "可以进入失衡状态…但物理层面上无法产生任何速度或移动…※与失衡免疫不同…※是否为静态刚体与单位的行动方式无关"; PRTS 失衡位移机制 (air units count, "但会因下述的判断而被取消位移"); every air unit of the mode except “炎佑” (weight 10, 我方单位) lists {{特殊机制|静态刚体}} in its PRTS 天赋, and so does 盐风主教昆图斯 — 28 keys (all 249 enemy pages checked). 失衡免疫 also: 刺胄之弹 / 斩胄之剑 / 破胄之锤 (their talent also lists 不可阻挡, so a grounded 剑 / 锤 stays unblockable); 守墓石像 转换模式 "无法被阻挡，自缚，失衡免疫，免疫浮空" and 飞行模式 "变为飞行单位，失衡免疫"; 喷气人 飞行模式 "近地悬浮，不可阻挡，失衡免疫，移动速度+50%", −90 % for 1.5 s after take-off and 1.333 s unblockable at −90 % after landing.
- **Now**: a static body moves 0 tiles from every push or pull; skills keep their reach and still hit it (薄绿 S2, 锏 S3, the hookmasters, 莫斯提马 S3 …; distance effects come to nothing). 守墓石像's statue is unblockable, rooted, `noDisplace` and refuses 浮空, its flight keeps a persistent `noDisplace`; 喷气人's 7 s flight is an air unit with `noDisplace`, then the brake and landing windows. Ground enemies keep §20.3's tables; 吉兆飞鳞 / 掠海漂移体 are 失衡免疫 while hovering and movable once grounded.
- [ASSUMED]: a static body's 0.1 s 失衡硬直 is not modelled; a 浮空 that 守墓石像 carries at its knock-out runs out; 见行者 S2 / 歌蕾蒂娅 S3 reach air units, 乌尔比安 S1 does not.
- Tests: `test/sim/feedback1b-displacement.test.js` (the data guard, 薄绿 vs drones, the act2 m01 flight, the 16 × 5 audit, coins / 流形 / links, the 胄 shell, 喷气人, 守墓石像).

### 21.10 Damage broadcasts in the Hidden Core; the strip by priority (C1) — `match/Match.js startFinalAssault / tickerText FLOW_TICKER_PRIORITY`, `match/finalAssault.js BOSS_HIT_STEPS / SharedBossPool.byPlayer`, `ui/ticker.js`, `main.js` (m.ticker)

- **Seen**: "隐藏boss还没打就出了造成50%伤害播报". **Reproduced** in real bot matches into the 隐秘核心: solo 绝境 seeds 3–5, the hidden leader's first hit (seed 3: 2034 of 900000) announced "超过50%"; co-op 2 seats 险境 seed 3: "超过80%" at 36 % dealt.
- **Cause 1 (server)**: share = `stats.bossDamage / pool.maxHp`, and `stats.bossDamage` (the result's 领袖伤害) sums both boss rounds. **Cause 2 (client)**: the strip played its queue first in, first out, 5.2 s a line; a Final Assault line played 2–8 s into the Hidden Core over its fresh leader.
- **Official** [DATA]: activity_table `autoChessData.broadcastList` comment_boss_hit_1..3 "{0}博士对敌方领袖造成的伤害超过20% / 50% / 80%!" (BOSS_HIT, paramList 0.2 / 0.5 / 0.8, priority 30); constData `broadcastBeginDelay` 1; client 2.7.71 keeps `bossDamage[localPlayer]` per boss battle (research 11 §2.1); research 06 §9.2 "The highest priority wins": BOSS_HIT 30 > CHAR_DAMAGE 20 > SHOP_LEVEL 11 > (bond 10) > GOLDEN_CHAR 2 > CHAR_GIFT 1.
- **Now (server)**: share = the player's damage to that round's `SharedBossPool` (`byPlayer`, fed by every credit path) ÷ its `maxHp`; each threshold once per player per round, a hit passing several announces the highest; `stats.bossDamage` unchanged (both rounds).
- **Now (client)**: lines keep `type`, `playerId`, `priority` and the round they arrived in; a BOSS_HIT line from another round leaves the queue and the strip; a player's newer BOSS_HIT line of the round replaces their older one, queued or on screen; and (integration, §21.19) the queue is ordered by priority, first in first out among equals, the line on screen never cut short, the lowest priority's oldest dropped past 4 queued — the remake's match-flow notices (隐秘核心已解锁, 联防阶段, a player out or gone) at `FLOW_TICKER_PRIORITY` 25 [ASSUMED], content `ticker()` lines 0.
- [ASSUMED]: the threshold test ≥ ("超过"); the per-battle reset of `bossDamage` (inferred); a dropped line is not replayed; the 1 s begin delay is not modelled.
- Tests: `test/match/bosshit-ticker.test.js` (solo 绝境 server-run, 2 and 4 seats client combat, SP_COMBAT=server, a takeover), `test/ui/bosshit-ticker.test.js` (the queue helpers across a 14 → 15 round change, the replacement rule, the priority order, timeline replays).

### 21.11 变形同构体 wearers in the member list and on the card (C2) — `ui/gameLogic.js grantedBonds / pieceBondIds / bondMembers`, `ui/bondStrip.js BondPopup`, `ui/detailPanel.js BondChips / ChessDetail / resolveDetail`, `ui/watchBonds.js ownerBoard`, `battle/runner.js ownerOps`, `sim/snapshot.js unitInfo`, `match/Match.js prepFieldMeta`

- **Seen**: with 变形同构体 (缪尔赛思's 特质 garrison_76 / band 变形者集群) and the matching item the wearer gets the bond's effect, but "那个盟约的人口数量没有把转职之后的干员算进去". **Partly real**: the count that activates the bond already included the wearer everywhere (`bondsMeta.pieceBonds` for the prep count, m.private / m.public, the battle input; `support.unitBonds` in the battle — checked on real matches for all 36 pairings). The client did not: the popup's member list and the wearer's card read only the records' bonds (在场 3/6 next to 成员 2/10, no chip).
- **Official**: band 变形者集群 "与特定装备一同装备时装备者将视为特定盟约成员"; eff_acarm073 "携带者获得额外盟约（由另一件携带装备而定）"; the item's talent lists the 14 pairings; a member counts on the board only (BOARD) and once per operator.
- **Now**: `gameLogic.grantedBonds` = the server's rule (a test pins the three together); the popup lists wearers tagged 同构, the card shows the granted bond as a dashed 同构 chip; a 同构 row opens the wearer's card with its item ids; a card without an own piece lists the carried items read-only under 装备. UnitInfo `items?` (sim `unitInfo`, `Match.prepFieldMeta`) carries an ally operator's item ids, so teammates' popups and cards show wearers too.
- [ASSUMED], unchanged: operator talents that test "【拉特兰】/【卡西米尔】/【叙拉古】… 干员" read the record's bonds (kits tier1 内线 ammo pick, tier5 inFaction, tier6 hasBond, tokens.js 卡西米尔 sword) — no source says whether a converted operator counts for them; 调和's +1 raises 在场 for core bonds without a member row (explained since §21.26: 含调和 +1 and a 调和 row).
- Tests: `test/ui/morph-bonds.test.js`, `test/ui/watch-bonds.test.js`, server-side locks in the match suite.

### 21.12 源石溶剂 is damage; 信仰搅拌机 S3 counters any damage (D1) — `sim/damage.js periodicDamage / isHpLoss / applyHpLoss`, `items/battle.js periodic_damage`, kits tier1 / 2 / 4 / 5, `content/enemies.js`, `bonds/addon/battle.js`, `choices.js`

- **Seen** (community): officially the 源石溶剂 drain sets off 信仰搅拌机 S3, and next to 莫斯提马 it stacks 拉特兰 on its own; the remake never cast S3. **Reproduced** on the real match path (R3; default S3 + 源石溶剂, 莫斯提马 above her, 隐现): v0.1.0 never cast S3 (SP full at 23 s); without enemies she drained to death with 0 layers.
- **Cause**: the drain was `loseHp` — a 流失: no 受击回复 SP, no TAKE_DAMAGE, ignored by shields — and the counter needed an enemy attack and a target.
- **Official**: PRTS 盟约记录 源石溶剂 "受到60真实伤害" (修正 "并非流失"; 备注 "造成无来源真实持续环境伤害" and "携带后，全场范围内的所有敌人类我方单位也会获得此装备的…效果（该效果的付与为我方阵营索敌，可对空，无视目标可选性）"); PRTS 作战机制 (生命流失 "不会触发反伤、受击回复"; "同名buff的默认叠加策略buff只能表现出一个"); PRTS 伤害分类 (BUFF damage includes "自残类型", "弧光锋卫失衡状态下的自残伤害", "深水区/涨潮水蚀"); PRTS 帮助 重装 "受到伤害时释放技能"; PRTS 信仰搅拌机 S3 备注 "反击受到任何伤害后均可触发，无需目标，视为普通攻击"; garrison_22 (莫斯提马): every 6 bullets spent on her 4 tiles → 【拉特兰】 +1 / +2.
- **Now**: `periodicDamage(n)` = 无来源 true damage (canDodge false, tags dot / periodic) with the old credit — shields, 脆弱, `hit` effects, 受击回复 SP and TAKE_DAMAGE all see it, the kill credit is unchanged. S3 counters every damage but a 流失 or an element 损伤, spending a bullet even with no enemy in range (measured without enemies: +5 拉特兰 per cast, elite 2 casts +22; with the R3 wave +7; the browser sim gives the same digests). The drain also ticks on every 敌人类我方单位 of the field (炎佑, a partner's too), credited to nobody, once per second however many carriers, while a carrier is on the field. Real damage instead of 流失 also for 狂暴宿主组长 (悬赏·特异III), 码头水手's drowning ("每秒受到1000点无来源真实伤害"), 弧光锋卫's 失衡 bleed and the 孽罪奇美拉 aura (now radius 1.2, a tick every 0.5 s, one tick per unit — "同类效果取最高"). One rule both ways (`isHpLoss`): 雷蛇 战术防御 and 蒂比 S2 react to real damage; 乌尔比安, 伪装服, 远牙, 录武官's guard, 坚守 thorns and 机变 自愈 skip a 流失; operator 流失 (宴, 风丸, 水月, 华法琳, 烛煌, 史尔特尔, 号角 …), 传递 and the drone link stay 流失. Damage dealt (unit `dmg`, the results' 造成伤害) never counts the source's own side.
- [ASSUMED]: the drain gives 受击回复 SP; the chimera aura is 无来源; a target-less counter is one bullet with no hit; the 炎佑 tick ends with the carriers; drowning keeps its 0.25 s cadence and the 失衡 bleed stays per distance moved (the amounts are official); 心烛 and 扎罗's transfers stay 流失.
- Tests: `test/content/feedback1d-solvent.test.js`, `items.test.js`, `kits_t1t2.test.js` (蒂比).

### 21.13 野鬃's push direction (D2) — `kits/tier1.js` (野鬃), `kits/tier3.js` (琳琅诗怀雅 S3), `content/generic.js`, `sim/Battle.js push`, `constants.js`

- **Seen** (community): "干员野鬃的技能有时会把怪物往攻击方向相反方向推". **Real**: S2 夹枪冲锋 pushed radially at full force (§20.3's table), so a target behind her centre — an enemy that walked through her tile while her single block was taken, grabbed by the §19.2 hand-over — was thrown 1.7–2.14 tiles towards the objective. Measured on act2 m01–m04 (R3 / R5 / R7 official waves, every melee tile × 4 facings): 2689 of 15915 pushes (16.9 %) went more than 0.5 tile against her facing (LEFT 47 %).
- **Official** (client data): her S2 attack ability (charpack char_496_wildmn, anim Skill_2) carries buff `wildmn_s_2[force]` of template `knockback[dir]` = Knockback {`_useSourceDirection` true, `_decreaseForceLevelWhenNotInDirection` 2} (battle/buff_template_data.json): a 方向力 along her deploy direction with the PRTS 推与拉 特殊修正 (> 45° off or < 0.25 tile → radial at 受力等级 −2). Radial = `knockback[relative]`: 琳琅诗怀雅 S3 (`swire2_s_3[knockback]`; PRTS 备注 "推开效果为径向推动"), 山 S3, 莫斯提马 S3. 圣聆初雪 S1 = KnockBackWithCharacterDirection (no correction). Cross-check: 莱伊 S1 `ray_s_1` decreases by 1, matching its PRTS 备注.
- **Now**: 野鬃 S2 = `Battle.push` with `dir` = `unit.fwd` (within 45° the full 中力 table along it: w0 2.14, w1 1.7, w2 0.44, w3 0.12; behind, beside or < 0.25 tile radial at −2: w0 0.44, w1 0.12, ≥ w2 0) — 0 pushes over 0.5 tile against her facing in the same sample; 琳琅诗怀雅 S3's coins are radial from her centre at full force (they went along her facing). The generic kit's text keywords (now also 往攻击方向) are a fallback only. §20.3's table is rewritten accordingly; nothing goes into [ASSUMED].
- Tests: `test/sim/feedback1d-push.test.js` (4 facings × ahead / ≤ 45° / behind / beside / < 0.25 tile, the mirrored boss half, a real act2 m01 R5 hand-over, 琳琅 S3).

### 21.14 Special Originium slugs and 暴鸰's bomb (D3, D4) — `tools/local-extract/extract.py ENEMY_SPINES`, `tools/assets/local-enemy-spines.json`, `plan.mjs localEnemySpines`, `fetch-assets --local-spines`, `assets.js spineEntry`, `render/units.js ALIAS_TINT / FORMS`, `content/enemies.js kitBombd`, `snapshot.js form`, `style.js PROJ.droneBomb`

- **D3** ("所有特殊源石虫的模型全表现为普通源石虫"), partly real: 高能 / 冰爆 / 简饲源石虫 and “庞贝” always had their own models; 灼热 / 炽焰源石虫 (enemy_1305_mhslim / _2, the ELEMENT slugs, up to 10 a round) were the plain 源石虫 — no web dump has their models (the asset plan aliases them); the official skeletons exist only in the client (refs/arts/enm_art_12.ab).
- **D3 now**: an optional overlay — the manifest keeps the alias as the web model and `enemies[id].spineLocal` names the files of the data/local-assets.json group `spine/enemy/<id>` with the metadata of the committed `tools/assets/local-enemy-spines.json` (data/assets.json is byte-identical with or without the extraction). The client draws the official model when the local manifest lists every file, else (or on a load failure) the web alias tinted toward the slug's own lava colours (`ALIAS_TINT`: 灼热 orange, 炽焰 red-orange, research 07 §5.6) [ASSUMED look]. The 0.1.1 release bundle carries the models (extracted with `node tools/setup.mjs --local`, the `spine/enemy` job). Audit (249 enemies vs the client's prefabs): no other enemy is drawn with another's model; 伊利昂的木驮兽 starts on skin `Full` in the game and is drawn with `default` (unchanged). `fetch-assets --prune` never deletes `public/assets/local` any more.
- **D4** ("暴鸰的炸弹无法正常投放"), partly real: the bomb hit in the trigger tick, with no projectile, and stayed under the drone. **Official** (prefab enemy_1040_bombd / projectile_bombd; PRTS 暴鸰): Boomb plays Attack, the bomb leaves on OnAttack (frame 8) and flies homing at 5 tiles/s; the cast ends when the bomb is gone, ≥ 0.667 s after the release; buff bomb_s: the bomb-less mode S1 (*_2 clips) + speed ×2 ("技能结束后"); `_immuneStunWhenAffecting` 0.
- **D4 now**: the drone holds still at the trigger; 8 ticks later (8/30 s) the release ('atk' droneBomb + the form 'bombed' — UnitInfo `form`); on landing the target 100 % ATK + the 8 tiles around 100 % splash (a target gone mid-flight: the bomb lands where it was; the drone's death after the release does not stop it); at max(landing, release + 0.667) ×2 speed and it flies on. A stun / freeze / sleep before the release interrupts the cast (no drop; the skill re-arms with its 1 s cooldown).
- [ASSUMED]: no drop by a dead drone; the ATK at the release; holding still through the cast; the bomb-less look from the release; an interrupted cast keeps its one trigger; a stun after the release does not stop the cast end; the trigger radius 2 (§20.4).
- Tests: `test/feedback1d-models.test.js`, `test/sim/feedback1d-bombd.test.js` (a real wave), `test/render/feedback1d-bombd.test.js`, `test/static-local-art.test.js`.

### 21.15 Enemy ground routes: 战场#04's official diagonal (D5) — `server/sim/grid.js` (`_buildField`, `_spfa`, `smoothChains`, `crossTiles` / `segmentTiles`), `data/stages.json groundPaths*`, test/sim/pathing-official / -blockable / -crosscheck / pathing

- **Seen (real)**: on 战场#04 活性源石 (act1autochess_m04, the only stage with tile_infection) the lower-gate enemies walked (9,10) → (9,8) along row 9, then straight up into (10,8).
- **Official**: every autochess route has `allowDiagonalMove` true and `visitEveryTileCenter` / `visitEveryNodeCenter` false; normal WALK routes have no checkpoints; the client SPFA flow field + Bresenham smoothing (research 08 §3.1; PRTS 作战机制 "所有敌人始终选择最短路径行动…") gives (9,10) → (10,7) → (10,4) → (9,4) → E — a diagonal that touches the floor (10,9) only at a corner.
- **Cause**: playtest #2's road-over-floor smoothing (§14 corrections) counted a diagonal step's corner tiles as floor and bent official diagonals into L shapes even where they cross no floor.
- **Now**: each flow field keeps two pointers per tile with the official distances — the official one, and 0.1.0's playtest #2 preference one restored exactly; `next` is the preference pointer only if its route crosses strictly fewer non-blockable tiles (exact geometry: a segment crosses the tiles whose interior it passes through, a corner touch is not a crossing), ties keep the official pointer (unless the preference one only skips a collinear official waypoint). Kept (playtest #2): 战场#01's lower-gate enemies take the col-8 road; on 战场#02's Final Assault both halves leave the exit via (2,9) / (2,11), so a blocker beside the exit holds them.
- **Audit** (11 stages × 14 gate / field legs = 154 routes): 140 official as merged by the workstream (0.1.0: 109); with the 深水区 refusal (§21.3) 7 routes of the inactive 战场#05(上半), whose canoe water now counts as non-blockable, differ too — 21 of 154 (pathing-official `DEVIATIONS`), each crossing strictly less floor. **Visible**: the boss exits of 战场#04, #05(下半) and #07(下半) are official diagonals — at #07(下半) the enemy passes (2,9) / (2,11) at √0.5 ≈ 0.70711 > the block radius 0.70709997 (§19.2), so an operator there no longer blocks them, as with the official route.
- Unchanged: movement tile centre to tile centre along `next[]` [ASSUMED simplification]; `findPath` / `groundPathTiles` return Bresenham tiles. One-line flip: always `use = o` in `_buildField` (pure official routes; reverses 战场#01's col-8 road).

### 21.16 The live attack range on the card; 烛煌 S3 (E1) — `sim/Battle.js _refreshRange`, `sim/skills.js changesRange`, `shared/loadoutRecord.js attackRangeGrid / traitRangeExtend / extendedGrid`, `shared/protocol.js unitStatsEntry`, `ui/detailPanel.js`, kits tier4 (信仰搅拌机) / tier5 (烛煌) / tier6 (荒芜拉普兰德)

- **Seen**: "干员烛煌开启3技能时候攻击范围不会变". **Partly real**: the sim was right (real 绝境 matches: 80 of 152 S3 hits on tiles only 4-11 reaches, none on 4-11 missed; the cast keeps the initial 3-1 per §20.2), but the battle card's 攻击范围 mini-map stayed 3-1 while the live ATK / interval beside it changed — the battle board has no range overlay.
- **Official**: skill_table skchr_blaze2_3 rangeId 4-11; PRTS 烛煌 S3 备注 "攻击溅射半径1.7；于攻击造成伤害前判定元素爆发并造成元素伤害", "补充后的弹药数量无法超过上限"; PRTS 信仰搅拌机 S3 备注 "此技能的攻击范围不受“攻击距离”属性影响".
- **Now (card)**: `unit.liveRangeGrid` (the running skill's grid, else the unit's own, plus `rangeExtend`) → `unitStatsEntry.range` (allies only) → drawn in battle 4× a second and in the prep preview; 400+ tiles read 全场, a grid beyond 6 × 7 draws smaller cells in the same box.
- **Now (record range)**: `attackRangeGrid` = the range a unit is deployed with — a selected "被动效果：攻击范围扩大" skill (引星棘刺 S3 3-9), else a module's 攻击范围扩大 grid, else its own, plus the 特性's permanent 攻击距离 (`traitRangeExtend`: 信仰搅拌机 SPT-Y +1; 空弦 ISW-A works in 集成战略 only) — used by the card without a live entry, the board overlay, the deploy wheel, the server's `summonRange` and the bot; over all 1284 loadouts it equals the battle's range at deployment.
- **Now (kits)**: 烛煌 S3 = one target on 4-11 + a 1.7 splash (中点判定; it used to hit every enemy inside 4-11); the 灼燃 burst bonus is dealt before the hit, on main and splash victims, from the attack as fired (her last shot lands after `end('ammo')` and keeps it); each landed shot emits fx 'splash'; a burst refills ammo only up to 18 / 21. 信仰搅拌机 精锐 + SPT-Y + S3 was 11 tiles, now exactly 3-13 (`targeting.noRangeExtend`). 荒芜拉普兰德 S1's target grid only selects (`targeting.showOwnRange`: the card keeps 3-1).
- [ASSUMED]: 技能范围 areas (古米, 泥岩, 山 S3, 锏) show while their skill runs; content extra tiles (蕾缪安, 维娜 S3) are not drawn; the 全场 / 6 × 7 thresholds (远牙's line is 2.1 px per cell on an 844 × 390 phone); the bonus also lands on a hit that is then dodged.
- Tests: `test/sim/feedback1e-skillrange.test.js`, `test/ui/feedback1e-range.test.js`, `test/render/feedback1e-splash.test.js`, `test/ui/playtest2.test.js`.

### 21.17 Healers that block keep healing — not reproduced (E2) — `sim/ai.js acquireTargets`, `sim/skills.js _defaultCondition`, `Battle.blockedTargets`, `test/sim/feedback1e-healers.test.js`

- **Seen** (relayed): "走炎盟约的时候，如果是那种会到处跑的BOSS，被医疗阻挡之后，医疗不奶人了，会输出BOSS而且如果是那种强力击类型的技能也会在BOSS身上打出正确数值的伤害"; the user asked to check every healer under every bond.
- **Official**: PRTS 卫戍协议/帮助 (技能策略) "对于医疗干员（咒愈师分支除外），攻击目标为需要治疗的单位"; PRTS 仇恨: a medic's heal uses HP_RATIO_NOT_FULL_ASC (full-HP units excluded, itself counted); PRTS 选择器: blocked units are added only if the selector's side can pick them; 咒愈师 "攻击造成法术伤害".
- **Verified, not reproduced** (real Battle, skills made ready while blocking): the 炎 lineup (9 炎, tier 3, 2 炎佑) — 录武官 normal / elite × S1 / S2 holds 卢西恩 / 不祥幻影, casts while holding, deals 0 damage and keeps healing; every pure healer (医师 / 群愈师 / 疗养师 / 链愈师 / 行医: 26 records, 56 loadouts, each skill cast while blocking) × 23 bonds as a member, and 112 items: 0 damage; the 20 boss / hidden fields: 0 healer damage (healers block 卢西恩 / 不祥幻影 and a 碎铳之簧 with its shield down; the other leaders and parts cannot be blocked by a block-1 unit). 古米 S1 / S2, 塞雷娅 S1, 波登可 S1 heal allies while blocking; 瑕光 S1 / S3 strike + heal by text; 咒愈师 hit what they block (§20.3); 吟游者 make no normal attack.
- **Likely what players saw** (all official): 卢西恩 evades only while unblocked (in the 炎 run 11 % of hits dodged and 1,162 dmg/s while it roams, 0 dodged and 6,751 dmg/s while the medic holds it — the numbers come from the others and 炎佑); a blocking medic heals itself first when it is the most injured; 神经 bursts stun it for 10 s; operators do not turn, so its heal animation faces the boss.
- **Now**: no behaviour change; the rule is written in §5.5, SIM.md, the ai.js header, `Battle.blockedTargets`, targeting.js and PLAYING, and locked by tests. Not checked: whether 华法琳 S2's official 流失 can kill an ally (the remake's can; PRTS gives no non-lethal note).

### 21.18 阵法术师 / 轰击术师 strike every enemy in range (E3) — `sim/professions.js` (SUB.phalanx / blastcaster, `resolveProfile` rangeAoe), `kits/tier4.js` 卡涅利安, the 流形 copy (`content/tokens.js`, `kits/tier6.js`), `render/fx.js`

- **Seen**: "干员卡涅利安的攻击不是真群攻". **Real**: the trait "技能开启时攻击造成群体法术伤害" was one bolt at the priority target + a 1.1 splash — spread enemies took 1 hit of 5 per attack; on act2 m01 R10 the elite 卡涅利安 hit 2.3 of 12.4 enemies in range per attack, 薄绿 2.0 of 12.6; on a real Match every 阵法术师 and 轰击术师 hit exactly 1 target while 2.7–4.7 stood in range.
- **Official**: a 阵法术师 / 轰击术师 attack strikes every selectable enemy on its range at once, the same damage near and far — primary for the 轰击术师: PRTS 作战机制 §AOE伤害判定, a 锁定攻击范围 AoE "在攻击前摇结束时选取范围内的全体目标，同时造成伤害" (its example, 伊芙利特's 炎爆, is her next-attack skill "下次攻击造成…"); for the 阵法术师 Terra Wiki Phalanx Caster (secondary) and PRTS 林 S3 备注 (one normal attack can kill several); supporting only: PRTS 溅射半径一览 has no radius for either (it omits 撼地者 too). 扩散术师 keep a 1.1 splash around the target (格雷伊 1.0, §21.19).
- **Now**: phalanx and blastcaster are `rangeAoe` profiles — `resolveProfile` applies the flag after every override (kit trait included): `allInRange` + instant `'beam'` hits drawn as a line to each victim (no splash ring, no shells). Blocked enemies are struck too (§20.3); a stealthed one only when revealed or blocked. Per-hit kit effects run on every enemy struck (薄绿 S2 拖拽 — a 静态刚体 is not moved, §21.9 —, 卡涅利安 S2 停顿 / 束缚, 阿罗玛 浮空, whose first-attack mark is shared between 阿罗玛 since §21.19). 卡涅利安 (PRTS 备注): a charged S1 keeps the skill-off trait — the guard AND 不攻击 for the whole skill (players will notice); S3's ATK climbs in 1 s steps to +140 % / +200 % at 20 s; the charged mark stacks before the damage, one `billro:mark` per enemy (another 卡涅利安 only adds stacks), ending with its setter's skill. A 流形 copy of a 'beam' becomes a bolt.
- [ASSUMED]: no target cap (no source states one); damage at the attack instant (no wind-up modelled); the beam look.
- Tests: `test/sim/feedback1e-aoe.test.js` (incl. a real wave counting the damaged enemies per attack id); opt-in `test/render/phalanx-aoe.browser.test.js` (`RENDER_E2E=1`).

| Branch (pool) | Official | 0.1.0 | Now |
|---|---|---|---|
| 阵法术师 (薄绿, 蜜蜡, 卡涅利安, 圣聆初雪) | every enemy in range | bolt + 1.1 splash | all in range, instant |
| 轰击术师 (协律, 阿罗玛) | every enemy on the line | bolt + 1.1 splash | all on the line, instant |
| 扩散术师 (格雷伊, 莫斯提马, 莱恩哈特, 夕, 寒檀) | 1.1 splash (格雷伊 1.0) | 1.1 | 1.1, 格雷伊 1.0 (§21.19) |

### 21.19 Integration follow-ups (`feedback1`) — `render/interp.js`, `render/app.js`, `battle/runner.js`, `screens/game.js`, `sim/Battle.js foesInRadius`, `sim/professions.js`, `sim/constants.js CHAIN_RADIUS`, kits tier1–6, `content/{enemies,tokens,generic}.js`, `content/items/battle.js`, `content/bonds/core.js`, `ui/ticker.js`, `match/Match.js`, `match/bot.js`

The last review of §21.4's client fix, the cross-workstream notes and the last review rounds were checked on the merged branch; the worthwhile items with a primary source are done here (the rest: §21.20):
- **Form fx in the render engine** (review of the final §21.4 fix, major): `render/interp.js isCosmeticEvent` counted every 'fx' as cosmetic, so `processEvents` dropped one stamped > 1.5 game s behind the render clock and a full queue shed it — after a main-thread stall over ≈ 0.78 s real at 2×, on a watched replica after a hidden tab (catch-up frames of 8 game s), after Chrome's intensive throttling, and for the game screen's replayed early buffer (stamped 0). Reproduced in headless Chrome: 转译基底·α ended `dead:null:B_Die` — report #5's look. **Now** a form fx is state for the queue too; one handed out late switches the model without its telegraph, with its closing clip shortened by the lateness and without a change clip that would already have ended. The runner compacts an overflowing hidden-tab backlog (`compactHeld`) instead of re-entering the view, flushes the backlog and a last snapshot of a battle that ended while the tab was hidden, and the game screen buffers the frames of a re-sent field meta for the field on screen until it re-enters it. The browser test stalls the main thread (blocking 1 s long tasks; the old `sleep` left the renderer's ticker running).
- **Area damage and 隐匿** (PRTS 作战机制 §AOE伤害判定 "AOE的判定是对攻击范围内的每个可以被选中的敌人进行判定"; 隐匿 "隐匿状态下的单位一般无法被敌方的索敌机制和Buff选择器选中为目标"): an operator's radius area effects — profession splash, aftershocks, skill circles and zones, counting conditions — use `Battle.foesInRadius` (no untargetable, no unblocked unrevealed 隐匿 enemy); enemy-side auras / heals and physical collisions keep `enemiesInRadius`. The 逐火 embers and every 隐匿 enemy take less splash than in 0.1.0 (officially they took none).
- **Splash radii** (PRTS 溅射半径一览, 特性 / 特殊): 投掷手 0.9 (was 1.0), 链术师 jumps 1.7 (1.8; `CHAIN_RADIUS`), 格雷伊 1.0 (1.1); 迷迭香 S2 末梢阻断 1.5 (the table's skill row; was ×1.3 [ASSUMED]).
- **阿罗玛's 非首次标记** (PRTS 阿罗玛 天赋 备注 "…自身离场时移除自身已施加的标记，不同阿罗玛之间的非首次标记通用"): one mark per enemy (`aroma:bubbled`, the setter as source) shared by every 阿罗玛, removed when its setter leaves (two used to trigger on one enemy; E3 made it show).
- **奥术法阵 on the field** (PRTS 盟约记录 备注, the same as 源石溶剂's "…敌人类我方单位也会获得此装备的“造成伤害时使目标失去特殊能力5秒”效果"): 炎佑's damage silences its target for 5 s while a carrier is on the field [ASSUMED: it ends with its carriers].
- **重生 cooldowns** (PRTS 特殊机制 §重生 "重生结束时，重置自身的通用技能与当前形态的技能冷却为初始冷却"): every ability with a cooldown restarts from its initial cooldown when a 重生 ends (`rebirthCooldowns`: `reborn()`, the husk's 1 s 重生, the statue's instant one).
- **高准度伦蒂尼姆城防自行炮** (悬赏 target; PRTS 技能 "以攻击范围内生命上限最高的我方单位为中心，生成9格的炮击区域，6s内每0.5s对炮击区域中生命比例最高的我方单位进行攻击…(最多进行10次攻击)", 备注 失衡 / 晕眩 / 冻结 / 浮空 / 沉睡免疫 during the animation): the lock is in its range (it was any ally), the area the 3×3 tiles, 10 shots 0.5 s apart (were 3, 1 s apart [ASSUMED]), 失衡免疫 + those immunities over the bombardment [ASSUMED: the animation's length].
- **琳琅诗怀雅 S3's coins** (PRTS 备注 "前方范围2-4内的地面敌方单位与自身阻挡的所有单位"): range_table 2-4 in front plus the units she blocks (her 1-1 attack range before).
- **阿戈尔's devour** (PRTS 盟约记录 "造成5000点物理流失", 修正 "【吞噬】的物理流失来源为被付与目标自身"; PRTS 作战机制: a 物理流失 "会受到目标当前防御力…影响而相应衰减"): 5000 less the target's DEF, still a 流失 (no shields, dodge or multipliers), the kill to the marker (DEF-free [ASSUMED] in 0.1.0).
- **The ticker by priority** (research 06 §9.2, WJ's note): §21.10.
- **AI 托管 and temp** (the promise "你开 AI 托管时，它不会销毁你的道具"): on a human seat under AI 托管 an item left in temp with a full hand gets a hand slot (a bench operator sold) instead of being destroyed; only a hand of nothing but items still drops it, as the temp deadline would.
- **The bot** (§21.6): reconciled with the merged rules as listed there.
- **0.1.0 bug found in the merge**: a 联防 battle timed out during 隐德来希 S3 reported its 心烛 as leaks, so the server refused the honest client's result (fullmatch-coop3 seed 116) — candles carry `mem.noLeak` and `Battle._timeout` skips them.
- **Checked, no change**: 斩胄之剑 / 破胄之锤 stay unblockable while grounded (their PRTS 天赋 lists 不可阻挡); the database's lpr-0 enemies (简饲源石虫, 节日气球, leader parts, tokens) still cost 0 LP in a leader round (the official `lifePointReduce`); 鸭爵 swaps skip bounty-tagged spawns; 转译基底's bounty coin is paid once.
- **After the independent QA of the merged branch** (residuals, each checked against its source first):
  - **WB's 22-match follow-up merged** (QA major): on the 11-match model 16 of the 58 official bounty drafts of the 22 matches could not be generated (two series-20 cards in an R3 set, named bosses apart at R9, an R11 draft without a giant); with the follow-up every one is reachable — §21.2.
  - **A status of the knocking-out hit** (§21.4): 妮芙 S2's fear (and a 叙拉古 6 proc, 铃兰's slow…) landed after the 重生's cleanse — the same hit runs `if (target.alive) applyStatus` after the kill, and the husk is still alive — so an ember walked unblockable + 隐匿 for ≈ 3 s more. A unit both 无敌 and 无法选中 now takes no status from the other side (PRTS 无敌 "无法被不同阵营选中"; `Battle.applyStatus`).
  - **余 S2 and 自缚** (in 0.1.0 already): 厚礼上宾 teleported a 守墓石像 in its 转换模式 onto 余's tile. PRTS 余 S2 备注 "处于消失状态的/持有自缚的单位不视为可达目标"; the statue's PRTS 天赋 lists 自缚. Flag `selfBound` (the statue, the 自缚 leaders, the 胄 parts' anchor) beside `noMove` — not `noMove` itself, which 束缚 sets too.
  - **信仰搅拌机 S2** (in 0.1.0 already, [ASSUMED] then): with fewer than 30 bullets the lethal hit went through. PRTS 备注 "弹药量不足时仍可抵挡致命伤害，此时将消耗所有剩余弹药并退出技能状态" and "自身持有不死时此效果不会生效": it blocks, spends what is left and ends; while 坚固维式重锤's 不死 window holds her (`items/battle.js holdsUndying`: this deployment's window) it steps aside (骑士戒律's earlier 不死 already did).
  - **Late form fx after a hidden tab or a catch-up** (§21.4's client half): the runner sent the hidden-tab backlog and a catch-up frame's events stamped with the frame's game time, so the render engine saw a 16 s old form fx as on time and replayed the 2 s change clip on a walking 转译基底·α (the final form was right). Such a step now drains its events every `EV_SLICE` (15) ticks and each batch keeps its own game time (`battle/runner.js stepEntry / drainSlices / heldAt`); render/app.js then skips the change clip as for a stall.
  - **The bot's bench shed** (§21.6): the buy loop's pre-emptive shed (one hand slot left) sold the single it had just bought for 3–5 back for 1 — the buy picks by lineup gain, the shed by piece value (co-op 绝境, 2 AI 托管 + 2 bots, seeds 21–26: 29 same-prep buy → sell, all from that line, vs 7 in 0.1.0). It now never sells a piece that came this prep (`sellWeakestHand keepFresh`); 3 remain, from other paths (a reward pick's room, `arrange`, `sellJunk`).
  - **Checked, not changed**: the QA's other notes are on the user-facing list (wording) or the release packaging (the 灼热 / 炽焰源石虫 models come from the gitignored local extraction: `public/assets/local/spine/enemy/enemy_1305_mhslim{,_2}` + `data/local-assets.json`).
- Tests: `test/sim/feedback1-followups.test.js`, `test/sim/feedback1-integrate.test.js`, `test/sim/feedback1-residuals.test.js`, `test/sim/feedback1-transform.test.js`, `test/match/feedback1-runner-forms.test.js`, `test/render/interp.test.js`, `test/ui/bosshit-ticker.test.js`, `test/match/feedback1-meta.test.js`, `test/match/bot-smarter.test.js`, `test/content/{enemies_bosses,bonds_core,kits_alt_t4,kits_alt_t6}.test.js`.
