- §21.10: ≥ for "超过"; the per-battle `bossDamage` reset; a dropped line not replayed; the flow notices at priority 25; no begin delay.
- §21.11: operator talents reading the record's bonds for "【X】干员" (a 变形同构体 wearer does not count there).
- §21.12: the drain's 受击回复 SP; the chimera aura 无来源; a target-less counter = one bullet; the 炎佑 tick ending with the carriers; the drowning / bleed cadences; 心烛 and 扎罗 as 流失.
- §21.14: the alias tint; 暴鸰's cast details.
- §21.15: tile-centre movement along `next[]`.
- §21.16: the 技能范围 areas on the card, extra content tiles not drawn, the 全场 / 6 × 7 thresholds, the bonus on a dodged hit.
- §21.18: no target cap; damage at the attack instant; the beam look.
- §21.19: the 奥术法阵 rider ending with its carriers; the 自行炮's immunity window = its bombardment.
- §21.21: a 突袭 retreat + redeploy is a deployment (it re-arms the lock); an in-place 复活 (M3茧甲, 埃芒加德) is a new deployment too (PRTS's 复活 is 退场 + a 0-time / 0-cost redeploy); the 阿戈尔 devour and the carrier's own 源石溶剂 spend the lock; kits' own savers before the item's 不死; M3茧甲 before 埃芒加德; a running window ends with its deployment and outlasts the lend that started it; a free pick or grant is no 购买; a 谢拉格 already priced ≤ 1 still counts as the round's first.
- §21.22: a ground enemy's shot in flight when 蒂比 takes off lands on nothing; ground-enemy auras still apply; 流失 DoTs keep ticking; an area skill cast because allies are near counts only those it can hurt (卢西恩's 【aoe】, 锏's CircleAttack).
- §21.23: air blocking (blockFly) on a fenced tile stays; the gate reads ground passability.
- §21.24: the body goes home once, at the knock-out, only when its home is free; 初始位置 = the homes of the board pieces of every player on the field, on the field or not (a summon that has expired or been killed included); 歌利亚头盔 ignores bodies; a withdrawn operator leaves no body.

Not done (noted, no primary source or out of scope): whether 华法琳 S2's 流失 can kill an ally (§21.17); 伊利昂的木驮兽's `Full` skin (§21.14); the render-demo recording `public/dev/recordings/normal-a1m04.json` still shows 战场#04's old L-shaped lower route (display only); M3茧甲's "同名装备的效果不可叠加" (two copies give two revives, a + b three) and two own 坚固维式重锤 giving a 16 s lock; the M3茧甲 / 埃芒加德 revive in PRTS's form (退场, then a 0-time / 0-cost redeploy: the SP reset and the other per-deployment effects — only 坚固维式重锤's lock treats the in-place revive as a new deployment) and a status icon for the 不死 window (§21.21); 深巡 S2's nearest-first order (§21.23); 【盲信之誓】 hurting once per chain (PRTS "多条锁链的伤害不叠加") and the “帝国的甲胄” barrage's timing (§21.22's audit).

### 21.21 坚固维式重锤's lock, once per deployment; 休露丝's discount (F1, F2) — `content/items/battle.js hammerAcquire / deploymentOf / holdsUndying / revivedInPlace / PRIO_REVIVE / PRIO_RESPAWN`, `content/bands/battle.js PRIO_BAND_REVIVE`, `kits/tier4.js` (信仰搅拌机 S2), `content/bands/meta.js` (休露丝), `match/PlayerState.js buy / pickReward`

- **F1 seen**: "维多利亚坚固锤子的锁血没生效". **Official** (act2autochess eff_acarm043 / eff_acgarm043): "首次受到致命伤害时生命值不低于1，持续8秒", `undeadable_duration` 8 (the latest upstream data agrees; PRTS still prints the act1 15 s); 异常效果 不死 "重设常规生命值时不会使其低于1".
- **Measured on 0.1.0, the lock works**: every HP loss of a unit without a boss pool fires `fatal` (hits of every type, element bursts, 无来源 damage, `loseHp` 流失) — on every visible chess against scaled real enemies, in forced-hammer bot matches and in the browser's local battle.
- **Why it looked missing**: it was once per battle — the hammer state lives on the unit, which a redeploy reuses, so a carrier knocked out after its lock came back with none (forced-hammer co-op matches, 3 绝境 + 4 困难: 27 of 382 carrier knock-outs); the 阿戈尔 battle-start devour (a lethal hit on the operator in front: 10 of 179 locks on 绝境) and the carrier's own 源石溶剂 spend it; with an M3茧甲 equipped first, its revive (same priority, registered first) took the first lethal hit.
- **Now**: once per **deployment** — the user's first-hand memory of the official mode (2026-10-03, "每次部署一次"; the text says only 首次 where M3茧甲 says 一场战斗 and 拉特兰桥夹 每次部署): a new deployment of the carrier (every deploy — the redeploy after a knock-out, a 突袭 retreat + redeploy, 阿戈尔's 立刻复活 — and an in-place 复活, below) re-arms the lock and ends a window still running. The `fatal` order is kits' own savers (10 … −60) → items' 不死 `PRIO_REVIVE` −100 → items' 复活 (M3茧甲) `PRIO_RESPAWN` −101 → 埃芒加德 −110: PRTS (M3茧甲 / 埃芒加德 / 阿戈尔 备注) "“复活”的实现方式为：受益者因移动之外的原因退场时下次部署的再部署时间和费用归零" — a revive acts on a knock-out, which a 不死 prevents. No status icon shows the 8-s window (the client has none for 不死; a one-shot fx marks the lock).
- **QA after the integration, fixed**: (1) the lock lived in the hooks of the carrier's hammer grants — a hammer lent by 萨尔贡 × 娜仁图亚 (60 s) took its re-arm hook along when the lend ran out, so a borrower knocked out while lent came back with its lock spent when lent again (real Match, 萨尔贡 6: no lock in the second deployment), and a window started through the lend ended with it (knocked out 4 s into its 8 s). The lock now belongs to the deployment — `deploymentOf` (the deploy counter plus the in-place revives) marks the deployment whose lock is spent — and one battle-level hook holds every running window (`holdsUndying`, also what 信仰搅拌机 S2 steps aside for): a borrower is re-armed in its next deployment like an owner, a lend refreshed in the same deployment re-arms nothing, and a window outlasts the lend [ASSUMED: the 异常效果 不死 outlasts its source]. (2) M3茧甲 and 埃芒加德 revive in place, which kept the lock spent, while 阿戈尔 5's 立刻复活 — a real redeploy — re-armed it; PRTS describes all three the same way ("“复活”的实现方式为：受益者因移动之外的原因退场时下次部署的再部署时间和费用归零"), so under the per-deployment rule a revive is a new deployment: both in-place revives now call `revivedInPlace` and the revived carrier locks again on its next lethal hit [ASSUMED].
- **F2 seen**: a 谢拉格 taken from the promotion reward used up 休露丝's "每回合购买的首名<谢拉格>干员消耗资金为1". **Not real**: only `g.buy` dispatches `onBuy`, where every 购买 counter lives (休露丝, 玛恩纳, 升华, 整备); the promotion reward, 寻呼模块's pick and the 拟态物质 / 紧急调度券 / 简易通讯机 grants never touch it (pinned through real intents; META `onBuy`). Buying the copy that completes a merge uses it, as it should.
- [ASSUMED]: §21.20.
- Tests: `test/content/feedback1f-hammer.test.js` (per deployment, a retreat + redeploy, the devour, the M3茧甲 order, the M3茧甲 / 埃芒加德 revives, a lent hammer), `test/sim/feedback1-residuals.test.js` (信仰搅拌机 S2 and the window of an earlier deployment), `test/match/feedback1f-sciurus.test.js`.

### 21.22 蒂比's 起飞 is 对地规避 (F3) — `sim/targeting.js evadesGround / canTargetAlly`, `sim/damage.js liftoffEvades` (DamageInfo `ignoreSelect`), `Battle.applyStatus / _blockerFor`, `buffs.js` (`liftoff`), `kits/tier2.js LIFTOFF_FLAGS`, `kits/tier5.js` (隐德来希), `content/enemies.js`, `content/bosses.js`

- **Seen**: after 蒂比 took off, enemies still hit her. **Real**: 起飞 was `unit.ground = false`, which freed her from melee enemies only; ranged ground enemies kept shooting her and their areas and statuses kept landing (46 of 241 enemy types hit her while airborne; the client replica the same).
- **Official**: ba.liftoff "不阻挡地面敌人且不会被地面敌人攻击，可以阻挡飞行敌人"; PRTS 术语释义: 起飞 includes 对地规避, a 无法选择 effect only selectors whose 行动方式 is ground respect, and she keeps a ground 行动方式; PRTS 异常效果: direct picks and abilities that "无视无法选择" are not stopped (these effects act "仅在选择时"); PRTS 作战机制: an AoE judges only selectable units.
- **Now**: 起飞 is the skill flag `liftoff` (S1 and S2, normal and elite). No ground enemy (not `Unit.isFlying`) selects her — normal attacks, ability picks, 周围四格 additions, chains, bounces, shells, the “帝国的甲胄” barrage, 鼠王's 沙狱, 烹泉's blast and steam, ground zones (集团军重型火炮's 燃烧区域 is "不可对空", 迷彩 alone exempted) and their statuses; the pipeline refuses a ground enemy's damage on her before the `hit` hook, so a refused hit neither sets off her S2 nor restarts 片场工作指南. She blocks flyers only; flyers, 近地悬浮 and 浮空 enemies still attack her with no 对空 check; she stays a ground unit (污染秽蚀 at the low-ground 50/s; 隐德来希 S2's 血镰 on her cuts air).
- **Still reaches her** (no selection — DamageInfo / `applyStatus` `ignoreSelect`, or 无来源): the "无视无法选择" abilities (【污染秽蚀】, 【盲信之誓】, 萨卡兹悖谬暴虐兵长's 暴击 splash — her 片场工作指南 may dodge it); 碎铳之簧's counter and 重装侦察兵's 暴露 on their attacker; the 刺胄之弹 / 剑 / 锤 blasts (flying units); the ticks of a debuff a ground enemy put on her before take-off (出血, 沙狱, burning, 淤困, 【自然涌动】 — their mods stay); sourceless damage (毒雾, the chimera aura, 源石溶剂).
- **QA after the integration, fixed**: 卢西恩，“猩红血钻”'s 【aoe】 (radius 2) and 锏's CircleAttack (first form, radius 1.5) decided to cast by counting every ally in their radius, so an airborne 蒂比 alone in range set them off and the blast skipped her (6 casts in 60 s, 0 hits on her); they count only the allies they can hurt (`!evadesGround`) [ASSUMED: no PRTS line states the trigger]. The player text (PLAYING §10, CHANGELOG) now says that ground-enemy auras / field effects and counters on her as an attacker still reach her.
- [ASSUMED]: §21.20. Audit: 蒂比 is the mode's only 起飞 / 对地规避 operator; outside F3, ranged enemies still pick invulnerable allies (锏 S3, 泥岩 S3) and enemy splash still hits 隐匿 allies (§19.1).
- Tests: `test/sim/feedback1f-liftoff.test.js` (14), opt-in `test/render/feedback1f-liftoff.browser.test.js` (`RENDER_E2E=1`), `kits_t1t2` / `kits_alt_t2`.

### 21.23 深巡 on a fenced tile; fenced units block no ground enemy (F4) — `sim/Battle.js _blockerFor`

- **Seen**: "干员深巡放在围栏围着的地块里不会主动攻击". **Official**: the fenced tiles are 围墙 (`tile_fence_bound`, "可放置近战单位，敌方无法进入") and 围栏 (`tile_fence`, "不可以通行") — LOW, buildable ALL, passable to flyers only; PRTS 围墙 / 围栏 地形机制 "部署在其中的单位，若当前阻挡类型为'地面阻挡'则无法阻挡敌人"; 深巡 is a 哨戒铁卫 ("能够阻挡三个敌人，可以进行远程攻击", range 2-2); defender skills fire "受到伤害时" (PRTS 卫戍协议/帮助).
- **Not reproduced**: from a fenced tile she attacks every targetable enemy on her range tiles — every deployable fenced tile of the 7 stages that have them, every direction facing walkable ground, normal and elite; the Final Assault halves and 联防; a real Match; the browser's battle (7 attacks, 1977 damage). Targeting reads range tiles only (no path or line-of-sight condition).
- **Likely what players saw** (official): in some directions her 3-tile line faces only fence or forbidden tiles; 阻隔工事 crates hold some fenced tiles from the start (act1 m01: 3 of each 6-tile block; act1 m04, act2 m03); nothing hits her there, so her skill rarely fired (since §21.29 her default S2 casts with an enemy in range, a deliberate deviation; S1 still waits for a hit); an own-tile melee unit never attacks from a fenced tile.
- **Fixed** (found while checking F4): a push or pull stops an enemy at the fence edge, 0.5 from the fenced unit — inside the 0.7071 ground radius — and 0.1.0 let that unit block it (薄绿 S2 on a fenced tile held the enemies she dragged). `_blockerFor` now refuses ground blocking from a tile ground units cannot pass (on the stages exactly the `b` tiles).
- [ASSUMED]: §21.20. Tests: `test/sim/feedback1f-fence.test.js`; PLAYING §10 tip.

### 21.24 Knocked-out operators keep their tile (F5) — `sim/Battle.js downOn / restTile / _layBody / isReservedTile / redeploy / _checkRedeploys / _downState / snapshot`, `sim/units.js body`, `content/bonds/addon/battle.js` (突袭, 不屈), `content/bonds/core.js` (阿戈尔), kits tier1 / 3 / 4 free-tile helpers, `render/interp.js`, `render/units.js setDown`

- **Seen**: "突袭盟约的干员会移动到濒死干员的尸体上". **Real**: a knocked-out operator left the occupancy map at once and only its home tile stayed reserved — a 突袭 member that fell after its jump lay on its landing tile, which the next jump picked; a timed redeploy came back on its home, where a teammate that had jumped there lay (96 real battles: 6 placements on a body; the shared boss field mirrored: 38).
- **Official** (PRTS 卫戍协议/帮助 §作战阶段 单位部署): "干员退场后…原地留下一个“倒地干员”…满足再部署条件时，移除场上的该倒地干员并自动部署至该位置"; "倒地干员所在地块视为可部署，但所有我方单位在此处的部署行为将被阻止"; "若干员被击倒的位置为其他干员或召唤物的初始位置，则在被击倒后，尝试返回其自身的初始位置". The 突袭 text "再部署至一名地面敌人周围" adds no tile rule.
- **Now**: a knocked-out operator lies on `body` — where it fell, or its own home when it fell on another board piece's home and its home is free (x / y / tile keep where it fell for the 被击倒时 handlers). Every redeploy comes back there (timed, 不屈, 阿戈尔); a withdrawn operator still comes back home. `downOn`: `_deploy` (redeploys, the 突袭 landing, summons), `spawnDevice` and `relocate` refuse the body tile for every ally; `isReservedTile` reports it, so the 突袭 landing, tactical points, summon / device pickers and 维娜's lions skip it. b.snap `down` carries the tile (§8.2) and the client draws the body there; WAIT_TILE matches the `_checkRedeploys` hold-back and is a safeguard. After the fix: 0 placements on a body in 526 jumps. **Superseded**: "later redeploys use the board tile" — an operator moved off its board tile (a 突袭 jump, 乌尔比安's anchor) now redeploys where it fell; research 01 §4.3 carries the PRTS quotes.
- **QA after the integration, fixed**: rule 3 counted only the board pieces that were not removed, but a summon leaves its home free only once it has expired or been killed (a docked one keeps its tile reserved), so the 召唤物 clause never applied — a 突袭 member that landed on the home of 浊心斯卡蒂's expired 海嗣 and fell there lay on it and kept the 海嗣 (远古血亲) from coming back. Every board piece's home counts now, on the field or not. Cost: the body bookkeeping added about 2.5 % per tick on 519 real specs (8 % on boss fields; whole matches and the perf budgets unchanged); `_checkRedeploys` now checks the DP before the tile scans, so an operator waiting for DP costs no ally scan — on the same 519 specs, against the integration head, the same tick counts and boss fields 11.9 → 10.9 µs per tick (a tile → body map is left for later).
- [ASSUMED]: §21.20. Tests: `test/sim/feedback1f-downed-tile.test.js` (rule 3 for a summon that has expired or been killed), `test/render/feedback1f-down-tile.test.js`, opt-in `test/render/feedback1f-down-tile.browser.test.js`; `downelem`, `bonds_addon`, `engine-requests`, `robustness` follow the rule.

### 21.25 Hardening credited to pull requests (PR #2, PR #7, PR #10, PR #14) — `match/effectsMeta.js dispatch / stillEquipped`, `tools/fetch-assets.mjs shrinkGuard`, `tools/assets/manifest.mjs droppedEntries`, `assets.js loadImageElement`, `ui/guide.js preload`, `css/screens/loadout.css`

- **The prep dispatcher's equipped items** (pointed out in PR #2, whose 突变细胞 bench rule §21.1 now follows — official footage confirmed it —, its merge path excepted): step 5 iterated `holder.items` live while handlers move and destroy pieces — normal 博士投影 destroys itself at the round start (the splice skipped the next item on that holder), 突变细胞 transforms its holder (the next item ran with a holder that was gone). It now walks a snapshot of the owned chess and of each holder's items and runs an item only while it is still equipped on its still-owned holder; a piece gained or equipped meanwhile waits for the next dispatch (effectsMeta header, META §2.2). No shipped item has a second hook on those events, so play is unchanged; a synthetic spy handler pins the rule (`test/match/feedback1-dispatch-snapshot.test.js`). QA after the integration: the items were read per holder as the walk reached it, so an item equipped meanwhile onto a holder not yet walked ran in the same dispatch and an item moved to a later holder ran twice (latent: no shipped item equips or moves items on those hooks); every [holder, item] pair is now taken before the first item runs, and two more synthetic cases pin it.
- **fetch-assets never shrinks the committed manifest by accident** (pointed out in PR #7, whose `data/assets.json`, regenerated on a machine where some downloads failed, was 42 audio entries short — all 42 still resolve upstream): a rebuilt manifest leaves out every entry whose files are missing on that machine. When it would lose an entry of the current `data/assets.json`, the run keeps the current file, prints the entries it would drop (also the report's `droppedEntries` / `manifestWritten`) and exits 1; `--allow-shrink` (or `--prune`) writes it (ASSETS.md; `test/fetch-assets-shrink.test.js`).
- **crossOrigin on the two `new Image()` loads** (PR #10, merged on GitHub): with `/assets/` served from another origin (a CDN or object storage) an image loaded without CORS approval taints the canvas and breaks the WebGL textures (an empty map, a white 3D board). `assets.js loadImageElement` — the asset store's only image path, §21.27's seeded store included — and `ui/guide.js preload` set `crossOrigin = 'anonymous'`; a same-origin page is unaffected.
- **The loadout screen's background layer** (pointed out in PR #14): `css/screens/loadout.css` `.lo > * { position: relative }` — one class, a later rule — overrode `.lo__bg { position: absolute }`, so the mint glow and grid layer was a 0-px flex item and never drew. Now `.lo > :where(:not(.lo__bg))`: the same one-class specificity for every other layer, never the background. Checked in headless Chrome at 1920×1080 and 844×390 (touch): the layer covers the overlay and every other box of the screen is where it was (to 0.1 px). Tests: `test/ui/loadout.test.js` (the cascade), opt-in `test/ui/loadout.e2e.test.js` (`SP_E2E=1`: the layer's box and stacking at both sizes).

### 21.26 Gaps found while triaging GitHub issues #1 / #8 (v0.1.1)

Where: `ui/gameLogic.js morphPairings / HARMONY_BOND / harmonyMembers`, `ui/detailPanel.js MorphPairings / MorphGrantLine / ItemDetail / ChessDetail / BondChips`, `ui/bondStrip.js BondPopup / BondStrip`, `match/bondsMeta.js computeBonds / bondList`, `shared/bandBonds.js` (→ bands.json `bondIds`), `match/gamedata.js bandBondIds`, `match/bot.js botPickBand`, `screens/bandDraft.js BandOffTag / BandOffNote`, `ui/gameLogic.js bandOffBonds`, `tools/botbench.mjs` (`--band`), `match/fields.js layerAllowanceOf`, `public/dev/game-mock.js` (variants `funny` / `morph` / `harmony`). Three gaps the user decided to close in 0.1.1 (no rule changes), and a false rejection of honest client results found on the way.

- **变形同构体 shows its pairings** (issue #1: players read the "转职球" as doing nothing). Its text says "具体对应关系可在模拟中查看本装备天赋栏", and no screen showed that 天赋栏. **Now** the item card (shop, reward, hand / temp: `ItemDetail`) has a 天赋 section: "搭配以下装备时，携带者视为对应盟约的成员：", then one line per bond in bonds.json order — 【bond】 and the items whose `giveBondId` is it (one name per item family, by tier): the 14 pairings of the official talent (character_table trap_1073_acarm073, "“炎国短刀”→【炎】盟约 …"; a test checks the two against each other), its "“维式重锤”系列装备" as the 5 hammers — 18 items, built from the same `giveBondId` the server counts with (`morphPairings`; `pieceBonds` / `grantedBonds`). A bond the mode never activates is struck through with 本局禁用 (`modeOffBonds`, §21.7; 标准: 拉特兰 阿戈尔 卡西米尔 奥术 突袭). On a wearer's card (its 装备 rows; also a teammate's unit and the card a 同构 row opens) the pairing it wears is highlighted — 生效中, or 已搭配 when that bond is off — and a wearer without one reads 暂未生效. A bond item's card and row say "与变形同构体一同装备时，携带者视为【X】成员" (`MorphGrantLine`). The 机密商店 cards have no detail view and keep the official text. Phones: the list keeps ≥ 9 px (px floors in `game-panels.css`).
- **调和's +1 explained** (issue #1: "一些不考虑整备区的盟约计算人数时也会把整备区的干员算进去" — the +1 read as a miscount; §21.11 had left "调和's +1 raises 在场 for core bonds without a member row"). The rule is unchanged (§6.3; one +1 total, research 02 §2.1 [ASSUMED]). **Now** a bond state whose count holds the +1 carries `harmony: 1` (`computeBonds`) and both views send it — m.private `bonds[]` and m.public `players[].bonds[]` entries `{ …, harmony? }` (absent otherwise; not in the battle input, `bondSnapshot`) — so the client never re-derives the rule. The popup says 在场 n（含调和 +1） and a 调和 row heads its member list, naming the 调和 operators on that board (`harmonyMembers`: 缪尔赛思, Pith's 盟约·辅助干员; a tap opens the first one's card; with that board not on screen "调和已激活：核心盟约激活人数 +1") — for your own strip and a teammate's (§20.15: the entry from m.public, the operators from the field on screen). The strip disc's and the card chip's titles add （含调和 +1）.
- **Bots skip strategies built around a switched-off bond**: in 标准 the bot took 潘格尼尼 (【定制铳械】 an elite <拉特兰>), 克莱门莎 (<阿戈尔>) or 玛恩纳 (<卡西米尔>) in 5 of 200 solo and 23 of 800 co-op picks (seeds 1–200). bands.json `bondIds` holds a band's tie, read from its own data at build time (`tools/build-data.mjs` through `shared/bandBonds.js`; `GameData.bandBondIds` and the client read the field) — the bond names in <…> of its text (the official note "在<X>部分干员缺席时体验可能不完整" among them; a bracketed item or enemy adds nothing) and the bond ids / bond pools of its blackboards (`bond`, `bond_id`; `pool` → choices.json `pools[pool].bond`, 潘格尼尼's pool_char_later) — nine bands are tied (杜遥夜 炎, 佩佩 / 娜仁图亚 萨尔贡, 哈洛德 维多利亚, 休露丝 谢拉格, 潘格尼尼 拉特兰, 克莱门莎 阿戈尔, 玛恩纳 卡西米尔, 贾维 叙拉古). `botPickBand` weighs a band tied to a bond of `gd.modeInactiveBonds` 0 — one rng draw per pick, the default band when every offered band is excluded: 0 of 200 and 0 of 800 in 标准, while the other modes keep exactly the earlier picks (绝境: 5 / 200, 23 / 800). `tools/botbench.mjs --band` still forces any band.
- **The strategy draft marks them 本局禁用** for human players (the owner's follow-up): a band whose `bondIds` meet the mode's inactive bonds (`gameLogic.bandOffBonds`, the same field and rule as the bot — a test pins the two in every mode) carries a 本局禁用 tag on its card and, in the detail pane, "本局禁用【拉特兰】盟约，此策略效果可能无法发挥" with the bond struck through (§21.7's look); it stays selectable — the official game lets you pick it.
- **Found on the way — the client-result layer bound** (`match/fields.js layerAllowanceOf / validateClientResult`): with the bots' new picks the full-match suite's coop/FUNNY/107 (4 humans on AI 托管) reached a 6-谢拉格 freeze board at R12 (初雪 + 银灰: garrison_28, 50 % +1 per freeze in range, no per-battle cap in the data; 凛御银灰 hands out a 60 % +2) whose honest +112 谢拉格 layers passed the flat 60 + 4·12 = 108, so the server rejected the client and re-simulated (SP_VERIFY=all agrees with the client on all 57 battles of that match). Uncapped traits are common (菲莱 / 百炼嘉维尔 per skill, 斯卡蒂 per 2 kills, 莫斯提马 per 6 bullets …) and capped ones reach 100–200 (史尔特尔, 荒芜拉普兰德), so the flat bound now adds, per bond, what the player's IN_BATTLE layer 特质 can add to it — each trait of its units and each one their ADD_BOND traits hand out (once per operator), on the bonds it names, up to the cap the sim applies (`max_add_count_per_battle`, the handed-out 华法琳 trait's `GRANTED_CAP_OVERRIDE`); a trait without a cap — or a 魔王, whose +extra counts toward none — leaves its bonds bounded only by the 999 room. A forged gain on any other bond meets the flat bound as before.
- Normative lines rewritten with it: §6.3 (`harmony` in the views), §6.6 (the band pick), §8.2 / §8.3 (`bonds[]` entries `harmony?`), §14 / §20.12 (the layer bound), §21.11 (its 调和 note).
- Tests: `test/ui/feedback1-gaps.test.js`, `test/match/feedback1-gaps.test.js`; opt-in `test/ui/feedback1-gaps.e2e.test.js` (`SP_E2E=1`; 1920×1080, 844×390, 756×366; `SP_E2E_OUT` moves the screenshots).

### 21.27 Client fixes for GitHub issues #5 and #8 (v0.1.1) — `ui/gameLogic.js prepCameraFor / foldCamera`, `screens/game.js`, `ui/fieldHost.js hudBands / cornerBand / seedAssets`, `render/app.js`, `css/devices.css`, `assets.js`, `render/units.js`

Two public GitHub issues after 0.1.0, both client-only. Normative lines rewritten with them: §9 (`setCamera` `shop`, the model fallback), §17.1 (the quiet budget), §19.8 (the collapsed shop).

- **#5 seen**: "准备阶段，收起商店界面时，界面并不会进行缩放（观战及作战时无此现象）". **Real**: folding the shop (收起) was UI state only — the own prep camera was requested once on entering prep, never with `shop`, so render/projection.js's official shop-collapsed camera (`left_prepare_camera_param` / `*_boss_prepare_*`, `presetCamera` `shop: false`) was never used, and `hudBands` always reserved the bar (measured: 110 px per tile at 1920×1080, 36 at 844×390, 31 at 756×366, the same folded).
- **Now**: while the own prep board is on screen and the bar is shown folded, the game requests `prepCameraFor(pub, myId, folded)` — the prep camera with `shop: !folded` — whenever the fold changes, with the normal 0.75 s flight (`foldCamera`): never over the pen (it folds the bar itself and returns to the camera it left), on a teammate's board, in battle or for an eliminated player (no bar); deferred while a piece is dragged or its direction is chosen (the drop target and the wheel sit on tiles of the camera in use) and applied when that ends; unfolding returns to exactly the shop camera; the fold still resets at combat start. The view hands the request's flag to `hud(kind, size, { shop })` (also on resize). The folded band is the higher of the tab (`.shopbar-tab`: .8rem + 3 px, above the bottom safe-area inset) and the corner buttons' hit areas (交流 / ⚙ / 📖 / ⛶, measured by `cornerBand`; on touch screens a 34 px button takes taps 5 px above itself, `--tap-min` 44 px): with the tab alone the corner covered bench pad 0 at 756×366 and the near edge of pads 0–2 at 844×390. Below 768 CSS px the corner wraps into two rows only because the open bar would cover it; with the bar folded it stays on one row (css/devices.css). Picking reads the camera of every frame. [ASSUMED] the folded band (the official collapsed HUD is not in the extracted bundles).
- Measured (headless Chrome; px per tile at row 10; every bench pad fully pressable open and folded, also the Final Assault prep halves): 1920×1080 109.8 → 130.7 (the official camera at its own scale, panned up 46 px to clear the tab); 844×390 35.9 → 44.3; 756×366 (touch) 31.4 → 40.1. A real drag deploy and a tap on a piece work folded; a drag or the wheel defer the flight; scouting (the 'normal' camera), the battle camera and the next prep are unchanged.
- **#8 item 5 seen**: "游览器切换页面后所有干员会变成方块+水滴的图标" — the image-less placeholder (render/textures.js) on every operator. **A plain tab switch does not reproduce it; a reload does** (phone browsers and Chrome's Memory Saver discard background tabs and reload them on return; the game resumes into the match): the asset store downloaded /data/assets.json a second time beside the game data's copy, `createFieldView` waited ≤ 4 s for it, every view looked its model and avatar up once in its constructor, and a failed download kept `{}` for the page — placeholders for good. Related without a reload: a battle that began in a hidden tab built no views (they are built in animation frames), nothing referenced a skeleton, and the quiet budget (§17.1) unloaded all of them ≈ 18 s later, so back in the tab every unit was an avatar diamond until its model downloaded again; a model that failed or timed out (20 s) was never retried.
- **Now**: the field host seeds the asset store with the game data's copies (`seedAssets` → `assets.seed` / `seedLocal`; the match screen already waits for them, so no second download). Without one, `ready()` retries a transient failure after 600 ms and 2 s (like data.js), then resolves `{}` without keeping it: the next `ready()` and a bounded background backoff (5 / 15 / 30 / 60 s) fetch again, and `onChange` tells the field view when a manifest arrives late — every view re-resolves what it could not draw (`UnitView.retryAssets`; prep item icons through `setPrep` / `ItemView.setIcon`). A view whose model failed or timed out loads it again after 2 / 6 / 15 / 30 s (`SPINE_RETRY_MS`; frames only, so never while hidden), `acquire(entry, { retry: true })` dropping the remembered failure; showing the tab again retries at once (also a missing manifest). Battle mode holds the Spine cache (`spine.hold()`, from `enterBattle` to the next `setPrep` or destroy): no quiet budget meanwhile, the 48 MB idle budget still applies, and the quiet budget keeps freeing everything in the lobby, room and result screen (the policy is in the assets.js header).
- Verified (headless Chrome, no console errors): a reload with every assets.json request delayed 6 s, with only a second download delayed (the 0.1.0 race: none is made now) and with the first 4 requests failing — operators drawn as Spine models at once; the first 6 failing — placeholders until the store's first background try 5 s later, then Spine models; a battle started in a hidden tab, back after 25–30 s (still running, ended meanwhile, or paused with Spine downloads slowed to 4 s) — every unit a model within 0.3 s of the return, no diamonds (before: the cache was empty from ≈ 18 s); six solo rounds with two battles started hidden — the cache follows the roster (4 → 24 skeletons, 6.9 → 25.2 MB estimated, idle ≤ 11 MB, under the 48 MB budget), never a diamond or a placeholder.
- Tests: `test/ui/issue5-fold-camera.test.js`, `test/render/issue8-placeholders.test.js`, `test/ui/playtest5-ui.test.js` (wiring), `test/render/assets.test.js`; opt-in `SP_E2E=1 node --test test/ui/mock.e2e.test.js` (fold / unfold at 1920×1080 and 844×390).

### 21.28 Match info in the strategy draft (GitHub issue #8 item 1, v0.1.1) — `ui/matchInfo.js`, `screens/briefing.js`, `screens/bandDraft.js draftInfoStatus`, `ui/enemyDrawer.js InfoTab`, `css/screens/draft.css .minfo-dlg`

- **Reported** (public issue #8, item 1, "选策略时没法返回查看禁用的干员和盟约"): the disabled bonds and the banned operators were shown only in the INFO_CHECK briefing, but a strategy is chosen partly by them. The owner took it into 0.1.1 as a remake addition (no official screen is copied; the look is the briefing's).
- **One component**: `ui/matchInfo.js` — `matchInfoModel(pub, { bonds, chess, mode })` (pure: `gameLogic disabledBondSets` over `config.modes[modeId].inactiveBondIds` — the drawn set D "阵容不完整" and the mode's static inactive bonds "本局禁用", e.g. 标准's 10; every bond by `bondOrder`, then `identifier`, split 核心 / 附加; the banned operators of m.public `bannedChess`, known chess only, by tier; banned members per bond) and the hookless blocks `MatchBondRow` / `MatchLegend` / `BannedOperators` / `MatchInfo` (greyed disc with ✕, the red badge, the `briefingBondTip` tooltip, the legend, the 本局禁用干员 grid). The briefing's right column is `MatchInfo`; the in-game 本局信息 tab (left 🔍) reads the same model for its compact chips and avatars (its banned list is now by tier, like the briefing's).
- **The draft**: 查看禁用盟约与干员 under the order list opens `MatchInfoDialog` — `MatchInfo` in the components.js `Modal`, read-only, closed by 关闭, a tap outside or Esc (a press on the backdrop closes it on `mousedown`, so the click never reaches what lies under it — e.g. 确认选择). The draft runs on underneath: the dialog's footer repeats the turn and its seconds (`draftInfoStatus`: 轮到你决策 / X 决策中 + the step header's number, orange at ≤ 10 s; 已选择「…」after the own pick); a turn change (a pick, a skip, a turn that runs out, an AI pick) or the own pick closes it; the end of the draft unmounts it with the screen; it never changes the highlighted band (`g.bandFocus`) or the buttons.
- **Sizes**: on desktop the briefing's own sizes (1920×1080: no scrolling); every size in the dialog is max(design rem, px floor), so on phones (1rem = 40 px; 756×366, 844×390) the text stays ≥ 9–15 px, the dialog fits the safe area and its body scrolls under a fixed header and footer.
- Tests: `test/ui/match-info.test.js` (the model, greying by state, badges, tips, the legend, banned by tier, real 标准 / 绝境 matches, `draftInfoStatus`, briefing / draft / drawer reading the one module); opt-in `test/ui/match-info.e2e.test.js` (`SP_E2E=1`: solo 标准 on desktop — dialog = briefing, the three ways to close, a confirm afterwards, the in-game tab; co-op 绝境 with a desktop and a phone — the live countdown, a turn change closing it, 844×390 / 756×366 layout and scrolling, the draft's end unmounting it).

### 21.29 Deliberate deviation: six 重装 skills cast with an enemy in range (GitHub issue #4, PR #12) — `tools/build-data.mjs TRIGGER_DEVIATIONS / resolveTrigger / validateAll`, data/chess.json `skills[].trigger`, `kits/tier1.js` (深巡, 雷蛇), `kits/tier2.js` (灰毫), `kits/tier5.js` (号角)

- **Reported**: public issue #4 — 深巡 (哨戒铁卫, range 2-2) never cast her default S2 行动能力剥夺 until something hit her, in practice until she blocked; PR #12 (merged on GitHub) gave 深巡 S2 and 雷蛇 S2 `trigger: 'DEFAULT'` in their kits. Many players asked the same of other defenders; the owner (2026-10-03): "反馈的人太多了".
- **Official, unchanged**: the 下半 (act2autochess, 2026-03) `skillTriggerDataList` row `TANK | | | 0 | TAKE_DAMAGE` and PRTS 卫戍协议/帮助's 重装 strategy "不受技能范围影响，受到伤害时释放技能" (下半, 2026/3/14) — a class row, covering every MANUAL 重装 skill whatever its index (§20.2); 上半 (act1autochess, 2025-11) had no TANK row, so 重装 skills used the basic strategy then (research 03 §1.4 keeps the history). PR #12's reasons — the row as "skill 1 only" and "offensive skills activate when an enemy is in their skill range" — rested on a community summary (arknights.wiki.gg), not on the CN client or PRTS; that quote is removed from research 03 (promised on PR #12).
- **Decision** (the owner, 2026-10-03): a deliberate, documented deviation — six offensive 重装 skills take the basic strategy (DEFAULT: SP ready, about to attack, an enemy inside the initial range or one it blocks): **深巡 S2 行动能力剥夺, 雷蛇 S2 反击电弧, 号角 S2 暴风号令 and S3 终极防线, 灰毫 S1 攻击力强化·γ型 and S2 专注轰击**. Every other MANUAL 重装 skill keeps TAKE_DAMAGE — 深巡 S1 侵袭破坏应对 included.
- **Where**: in the data, so the server, the browser battle and the bots read one rule. `TRIGGER_DEVIATIONS` is keyed by the normal chess id and the skill id (the elite record follows through its `baseId`), never by the skill id alone: 灰毫 S1 is the generic `skcom_atk_up[3]`, which 惊蛰, 幽灵鲨, 耶拉, 莫斯提马 and 莱恩哈特 carry too. `resolveTrigger` applies it last; the 12 records read `trigger { rule: 'DEFAULT', rawRule: 'TAKE_DAMAGE' }` — `rawRule` keeps the official row for traceability — and `validateAll` fails the build when an entry no longer meets a TAKE_DAMAGE row. PR #12's kit lines (`trigger: 'DEFAULT'` on 深巡 / 雷蛇 S2) stay, agreeing with the data (a test pins kit = data); their comments and the contributor's tests now give this section's reason.
- **Measured** (a real battle: 战场#01(下半), one 萨卡兹枯朽前锋 walking the lane from the gate, the operator SP-full on (9,5) facing it): 深巡 / 雷蛇 S2 cast 6.2 s after the spawn, when the enemy steps onto the 2-2 line, and 号角 S2 / S3 and 灰毫 S1 / S2 within 1.3 s (their 要塞 range reaches the gate) — each before anything has hit it (the enemy reaches the defender after ≈ 24 s, if at all); each of the other 46 MANUAL 重装 records (normal + elite) casts on the first hit it takes, at 23.8 s.
- **Players notice**: these six fire sooner and more often — from range, before anything reaches the defender; 号角 S3's 过载 HP drain therefore starts sooner; 灰毫 S2 (block 0) lets an enemy pass as soon as one comes into range, not after the first hit; on a fenced tile (§21.23) 深巡's S2 fires.
- One-line flip: remove the entry from `TRIGGER_DEVIATIONS` (for 深巡 / 雷蛇 also the kit line) and rebuild.
- Normative lines rewritten with it: §5.6 (`TAKE_DAMAGE`), §19.2 (灰毫 S2), §20.2 (players notice, the table), §21.20 (a settled row), §21.23 (深巡 on a fenced tile).
- Tests: `test/sim/feedback1-tank-triggers.test.js` (the data, kit = data, the real battle for the six and for every other MANUAL 重装 record), `test/data.test.js`, `kits_t1t2` / `kits_alt_t1` (PR #12's), `kits_alt_t2` (灰毫 S2), `kits_t5` / `kits_alt_t5` (号角), `test/docs-consistency.test.js`.
